Method: 提出MemDreamer框架，包含：(1) 分层图记忆架构（自上而下的三层语义抽象结构，基础图捕捉时空和因果关系）；(2) 智能体工具增强检索（在推理时通过观察-推理-行动循环，导航层次、搜索节点、遍历逻辑边）；(3) 增量式流式视频处理。

Result: 在四个主流基准测试上达到SOTA，与人类专家差距仅3.7分；推理上下文窗口仅占全上下文摄入的2%，准确率绝对提升12.5分；发现VLM的逻辑推理与长视频理解性能之间存在强正线性相关。

Conclusion: MemDreamer通过解耦感知与推理、引入智能体能力缩放，为多模态长视频理解建立了新范式，证明了智能体能力缩放是多模态理解的新方向。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language Models struggle with hours-long videos because processing full-length visual sequences induces prohibitive token explosion and attention dilution. To overcome this, we introduce MemDreamer to decouple perception and reasoning, shifting long-video understanding into an agentic exploration process. As a plug-and-play framework, it incrementally streams videos to construct a Hierarchical Graph Memory, a top-down three-tier architecture for semantic abstraction, anchored by a foundational graph capturing spatiotemporal and causal relations. During inference, the reasoning model employs agentic tool-augmented retrieval, navigating hierarchies, searching nodes, and traversing logical edges via an Observation-Reason-Action loop. Experiments show MemDreamer achieves SOTA results across four mainstream benchmarks, narrowing the gap with human experts to only 3.7 points. It constrains the reasoning context window to merely 2% of full-context ingestion while delivering a 12.5 point absolute accuracy gain. Furthermore, statistical analysis uncovers a strong positive linear correlation between an VLM's performance on logic reasoning and long-video understanding benchmarks, establishing agentic capability scaling as a new paradigm for multimodal comprehension.

</details>


### [75] [Geometric-Aware Hypergraph Reasoning for Novel Class Discovery in Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07280)
*Zihao Zhang,Aming Wu,Yang Li,Yahong Han,Jialie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超图框架和几何感知原型的方法，用于点云分割中的新类别发现，通过高阶关联建模和几何信息增强，在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖成对关联进行类别分配和新类别推理，难以捕获已知和新类别间的复杂关系，且对点云的几何信息关注不足，导致语义分割不准确。

Method: 1) 提出超图框架建模类别间的高阶关联，支持从已知类别到新类别的协作推理；2) 提出几何感知原型（Geometric-Aware Prototypes），利用超边传播几何信息，增强类级几何线索的表示。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 超图建模高阶关系与几何感知原型相结合，能有效提升点云新类别发现的分割精度，优于依赖成对关系的传统方法。

Abstract: Novel class discovery in point cloud segmentation aims to transfer knowledge from known classes to automatically identify and segment unlabeled novel classes in point clouds. Existing methods mainly rely on pairwise associations for class assignment and novel class reasoning, which limits their ability to capture complex relationships among known and novel classes and may lead to inaccurate semantic segmentation. To address this issue, we introduce a hypergraph-based framework that models high-order associations among classes and enables collaborative reasoning from known classes to novel classes beyond traditional pairwise relations. Moreover, existing methods tend to focus on semantic feature extraction while paying insufficient attention to geometric information in point clouds. To better exploit spatial structure, we propose Geometric-Aware Prototypes to enhance the representation of class-level geometric cues. By propagating geometric information through hyperedges, the proposed method improves the understanding of spatial distributions across classes and leads to more accurate segmentation. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI and SemanticPOSS datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method.

</details>


### [76] [ExMesh: EXplicit Mesh Reconstruction with Topology Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07288)
*Chuanjin Fan,Lifan Wu,Wenjie Chang,Hanzhi Chang,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExMesh是一个直接优化显式网格的框架，通过集成可微优化与离散拓扑更新，实现了自适应的顶点分裂合并和实时UV维护，无需中间表示和后处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（无论是隐式还是显式）都依赖于中间表示（如Marching Cubes或TSDF融合），导致伪影和碎片化几何。直接优化显式网格面临两个关键挑战：如何自适应细化网格拓扑以捕捉细节而不产生退化面，以及如何在网格结构演化时维持一致的UV坐标以实现高保真纹理映射。

Method: 提出ExMesh框架，将可微优化与离散拓扑更新集成到统一管道中。核心包括：自适应顶点分裂与合并策略（粗到细优化），以及实时UV坐标维护机制。

Result: 大量实验表明，ExMesh在精度、计算效率和网格简洁性之间实现了良好平衡。据作者称，这是首个将离散拓扑操作无缝集成到连续可微优化管道中的框架。

Conclusion: ExMesh通过直接优化显式网格并集成离散拓扑更新，克服了传统方法依赖中间表示的局限性，为多视角表面重建提供了一种高效、保真的新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing surface meshes from multi-view images has remained a core challenge in recent years. Most existing methods, whether implicit or explicit, depend on intermediate representations and post-processing steps like Marching Cubes or TSDF fusion, often resulting in artifacts and fragmented geometry. Directly optimizing explicit meshes is a promising approach. However, it presents two critical challenges. The first is how to adaptively refine mesh topology to capture detail without introducing degenerate faces. The second is how to maintain consistent UV coordinates for high-fidelity texturing as the mesh structure evolves. To overcome these, we propose ExMesh, a novel framework that directly optimizes explicit meshes by integrating differentiable optimization with discrete topology updates. Specifically, we introduce an adaptive vertex splitting and merging strategy, along with real-time UV maintenance, to enable coarse-to-fine optimization while preserving geometric integrity. To our knowledge, ExMesh is the first framework to seamlessly integrate discrete topology operations into a continuous differentiable optimization pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ExMesh achieves a balance among accuracy, computational efficiency, and mesh conciseness.

</details>


### [77] [AnchorWorld: Embodied Egocentric World Simulation with View-based Evolution Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07326)
*Yu Li,Menghan Xia,Gongye Liu,Xintao Wang,Conglang Zhang,Lei Ke,Yuxuan Lin,Ruihang Chu,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorWorld是一个交互式世界建模框架，通过引入外源视角辅助训练和锚定视图定制机制，在egocentric仿真中实现了更鲁棒的人-环境交互建模和可定制世界动态演化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界建模在实际场景中需要灵活的可控性，但现有工作在交互完整性和世界定制方面探索不足，尤其是egocentric视角下人体部位常被截断或超出视野。

Method: 1) 使用3D人体运动作为主要交互模态；2) 引入与agent第一人称传感器解耦的外源视角辅助训练，弥补egocentric视角中身体部位不完整的问题；3) 提出锚定视图机制，在世界坐标系中定义锚定视角，结合文本描述来定制局部场景的动态演化。

Result: AnchorWorld显著优于现有最先进基线方法，消融实验验证了关键设计有效性；定制方案展现出良好的时空几何一致性，并严格遵循预设的演化动态。

Conclusion: AnchorWorld通过外源视角监督增强了人-环境交互的空间鲁棒性，同时锚定视图+文本描述机制实现了灵活、一致的世界定制，为交互式世界建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite being a pivotal frontier, interactive world modeling remains underexplored in terms of the versatile controllability required by practical scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present AnchorWorld, a framework that advances egocentric simulation through enhanced interaction integrity and a flexible mechanism for world customization. First, we utilize 3D human motion as the primary interaction modality. To complement the out-of-view or truncated body parts in egocentric views, we introduce an auxiliary training supervision that incorporates exogenous viewpoints decoupled from the agent's first-person sensorium. It allows the model to observe the agent's full-body positioning relative to the environment, facilitating a more robust spatial grounding of human-world interactions. Furthermore, we propose a simple yet effective mechanism for customizing self-evolving worlds. This is achieved by defining anchor views within a unified world coordinate system, coupled with textual descriptions dictating the dynamic evolution of local scenes. Experimental results show that AnchorWorld significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, while ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our key designs. Notably, our customization scheme exhibits promising spatio-temporal geometric consistency and adheres strictly to the prescribed evolutionary dynamics.

</details>


### [78] [Varifold Moment Invariants for Sustainable and Explainable Contour Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07333)
*G. Longari,J. -C. Alvarez Paiva,A. B. Tumpach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Varifold Moment Invariants (VMI)框架，统一多种矩不变量方法，通过结合区域几何、边界和切线族生成高判别力几何特征，配合轻量分类器在多种数据集上以低计算成本实现优于现有轮廓方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩不变量方法分散且缺乏统一框架，不同轮廓特征（如扩展高斯图像、椭圆傅里叶描述子等）之间关系不明确，需要一种能整合区域几何、边界和切线信息的高判别力特征表示方法。

Method: 提出Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI)统一框架，将区域几何、边界和切线族信息融合生成大量具有清晰几何意义的旋转平移不变特征，配合Random Forest或MLP轻量分类器进行特征提取与分类。

Result: 在叶子、物体、细胞等多种公开数据集上达到高精度，以少量几何可解释特征超越现有基于轮廓的方法，同时计算成本大幅降低，可在轻量设备上运行。

Conclusion: VMI是一个统一且高效的矩不变量框架，兼具高判别力、几何可解释性和低计算成本，适合部署于轻量设备。

Abstract: We introduce Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI) as a unifying framework for many previously introduced Moment Invariants. These invariants are deeply related to other contour features that are invariant under translations and rotations, like Extended Gaussian Image, Elliptic Fourier Descriptors or Shape Distributions. The advantage of the varifold approach to moments consists in being able to combine the geometry of the region, its boundary, and the family of lines tangent to it, in order to create a substantial number of invariant features with high discriminating power and clear geometric meaning. By coupling our VMI feature extraction with the light feature classifiers Random Forest or Multi-Layer-Perceptron, we outperform state-of-the-art approaches based on contours, while decreasing drastically the computational cost to the point of allowing our algorithm to run on light devices. We tested our approach on classification tasks on a large number of widely-used datasets of various types (leaves, objects, cells) and achieved high accuracy with a low number of geometrically interpretable features.

</details>


### [79] [VeriDrive: Verifiable Counterfactual Supervision for Cost-Efficient Vision-Language Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07338)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. H. Shum,Toby P. Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriDrive是一个构建可验证、规划导向的反事实监督框架，将驾驶推理转化为结构化的感知-评估-修正链，通过局部生成与验证器引导的选择性修正来扩展数据构建，在nuScenes数据集上实验表明其在提升驾驶性能的同时降低了计算和标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言驾驶模型使用推理监督来连接感知、预测和规划，但已有的驾驶推理链通常是自由形式的，且使用前沿模型生成成本高昂。作者希望构建一个更结构化、可验证且成本可控的推理监督框架。

Method: VeriDrive将驾驶推理转化为结构化的"感知-评估-修正"链（Perception-Evaluation-Revision chain）：1) 将关键对象锚定在未来运动上；2) 使用可规则检查的证据评估替代自我轨迹；3) 将风险意图修正为专家行为；4) 产生最终规划目标。数据构建上采用局部生成与验证器引导的选择性修正相结合，仅升级处理无效或困难样本。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上基于Omni-Q协议训练，开环实验表明：VeriDrive在L2误差、碰撞率和交叉率上优于OmniDrive，同时降低了日志令牌使用量、生成时间和实际LLM/VLM付费成本。

Conclusion: 可审计的中间字段和结构化修正目标能够在现实标注预算下改进视觉语言规划监督。表明结构化推理监督比自由形式推理链更高效且经济。

Abstract: Vision-language driving models increasingly use reasoning supervision to bridge perception, prediction, and planning, but existing driving rationales are often free-form and expensive to generate with frontier models. We present VeriDrive, a framework for constructing planning-oriented, verifiable counterfactual supervision. VeriDrive converts driving reasoning into a structured Perception-Evaluation-Revision chain that grounds key objects in future motion, evaluates alternative ego trajectories with rule-checkable evidence, revises risky intent toward expert behavior, and produces final planning targets. To scale data construction, VeriDrive combines local generation with validator-guided selective correction, escalating only invalid or difficult samples. We build the VeriDrive dataset on nuScenes and train under the Omni-Q protocol. Controlled open-loop experiments show that VeriDrive improves L2, Collision, and Intersection over OmniDrive while reducing logged token usage, generation time, and actual paid LLM/VLM cost. These results show that auditable intermediate fields and structured revision targets can improve vision-language planning supervision under realistic annotation budgets. Code, prompts, and validator scripts are coming soon and will be released after the review process.

</details>


### [80] [Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter for Micro-gesture Online Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07355)
*Xucheng Shen,Kun Li,Fei Wang,Wei Qian,Jin Jiang,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空解耦适配器（STD-Adapter）和自适应软平衡增强（ASBA）方法，用于微手势在线识别，在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2中取得第一名（F1=0.43808）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手势持续时间极短、运动幅度小、视觉线索模糊，现有参数高效适配器采用单分支联合建模时空信息，难以捕捉细粒度模式。

Method: 提出时空解耦适配器（STD-Adapter），通过轻量级深度可分离卷积将视频适配分解为独立的时间分支和空间分支；同时提出自适应软平衡增强（ASBA），根据类别稀少性和学习难度动态分配增强强度，无需手动阈值。

Result: 在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2中取得第一名，F1分数为0.43808。

Conclusion: 时空解耦的设计能更有效捕捉微手势的细粒度时空模式，自适应增强策略有效缓解了长尾分布问题。

Abstract: Micro-gesture online recognition aims to temporally localize and classify subtle gestures in untrimmed videos. Owing to their extremely short duration, low motion amplitude, and ambiguous visual cues, capturing discriminative spatiotemporal representations remains highly challenging. Existing parameter-efficient adapters typically employ a single branch to model spatial and temporal cues jointly, which may fail to capture the fine-grained patterns of micro-gestures. To address this limitation, we propose a Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter that decomposes video adaptation into independent temporal and spatial branches via lightweight depthwise convolutions. In addition, to address the long-tail distribution problem in the benchmark dataset, we introduce Adaptive Soft Balanced Augmentation, which dynamically allocates augmentation intensity based on class rarity and learning difficulty, without manual thresholds. Our method achieves an F1 score of 0.43808, ranking 1st in Track 2 of the 4th EI-MiGA-IJCAI Challenge.

</details>


### [81] [Mind the Gap: Disentangling Performance Bottlenecks in Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07394)
*Danial Hamdi,Fardin Ayar,Mahdi Javanmardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断框架（ILP求解器）分离VIS任务中分类、分割和跟踪的误差贡献，发现跟踪不稳定是主要瓶颈（尤其在线方法），且强骨干网络无法弥补，并配套推出TrackLens可视化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIS任务中分类、分割和跟踪目标联合评估，但各自对性能损失的贡献不透明，缺乏系统诊断工具。

Method: 将身份和类别分配建模为整数线性规划（ILP），构建模型无关的Oracle，分层隔离各误差源；在7种VIS方法（离线/在线）上于YTVIS 2019/2021和OVIS子集进行分析。

Result: 在线方法中跟踪不稳定是关键瓶颈，重遮挡下AP差距超20；跟踪差距随视频长度和实例密度急剧增大；分类影响在跟踪失败时可忽略；强骨干可提升默认分数但跟踪差距基本不变。

Conclusion: VIS核心挑战在于鲁棒的长期时序关联，而非表征能力不足；TrackLens将差距转化为可观察的查询级失效模式，为针对性优化提供基础。

Abstract: In Video Instance Segmentation (VIS), classification, segmentation, and tracking objectives are jointly evaluated, but their individual contributions to performance loss remain opaque. We introduce a diagnostic framework that formulates identity and class assignment as an Integer Linear Program (ILP), yielding a model-agnostic oracle that hierarchically isolates each error source. Applied to seven VIS methods spanning online and offline paradigms across YouTube-VIS 2019/2021 and a diagnostic subset of OVIS, our analysis reveals a consistent picture. Tracking instability is a critical bottleneck for online methods, with gaps exceeding 20 AP under heavy occlusion, and grows sharply with video length and instance density. While semantic classification contributes meaningfully on standard benchmarks, its impact becomes negligible where tracking fails most. Although stronger backbones substantially lift default scores, they leave AP tracking gaps largely intact, confirming that temporal fragility is algorithmic rather than purely representational. To complement the oracle, we introduce TrackLens, a visual tool that translates gap magnitude into observable, query-level failure modes. Together, these tools provide a systematic foundation for targeting VIS's core challenge: robust long-term temporal association.

</details>


### [82] [RealDocBench: A Benchmark for Field-Level QA and Layout Understanding on Real-World Regulated Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07401)
*Ameya Joshi,Joon Kim,Gus Eggert,Joseph Bajor,Cindy Hao,Jing Reyhan,Kushal Byatnal,Eli Badgio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RealDocBench，一个基于真实监管文档的双轨基准（QA 问答轨 + Layout 布局轨），通过18个系统评测揭示了现有文档解析方法在真实场景下的性能差异、成本延迟权衡以及医学子领域的持续困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准多使用整洁的学术版面或合成文本，仅报告单一的 OCR 或 Markdown 相似度分数，无法反映下游智能体（如抵押贷款、财务报告）对真实复杂文档中特定字段值的实际需求。

Method: 构建 RealDocBench，包含两个轨道：QA 轨（1,356 个字段级问题，581 份文档，4 个领域，带类型化的键值答案）和 Layout 轨（1,500 张人工验证页面图像，9 类公开分类法下的 COCO 格式边界框标注，使用带邻域感知的分割/合并恢复的匈牙利匹配器评分）。采用统一的提取与评分协议评估 18 个系统（商业解析 API、通用 VLM、开源 OCR 模型）。

Result: RealDocBench 揭示了单一数字基准所隐藏的广泛性能差距；医学子领域仍然困难；不同操作点在成本/延迟之间存在明显的权衡。

Conclusion: RealDocBench 为文档解析系统提供了可复现的字段级比较基准，并公开数据集、解析适配器和评估工具以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Document parsing systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes, regulated workflows such as mortgage underwriting, financial reporting, supply-chain logistics, and clinical records. Yet most public benchmarks evaluate parsers on clean academic layouts or synthetic prose, and report a single OCR or markdown-level similarity score. Such documents and metrics correlate poorly with what downstream agents actually need: the correct value for a specific field on a messy real-world page. We introduce RealDocBench, a two-track benchmark built from real regulated documents. The QA track contains 1,356 field-level questions over 581 documents spanning four domains, where each question is paired with a typed gold_dict of key-to-value answers and parsers are scored on both per-field and strict per-question accuracy. The layout track contains 1,500 human-verified page images annotated with COCO-style bounding boxes under a nine-class public taxonomy, scored with a Hungarian matcher that includes adjacency-aware split/merge recovery. We evaluate eighteen systems, spanning commercial parsing APIs, general-purpose VLMs, and open-source OCR models, under a uniform extraction-and-scoring protocol, and report accuracy alongside per-page cost and cache-busted latency. RealDocBench exposes a wide performance spread that single-number benchmarks hide, a persistently hard medical sub-domain, and sharp cost/latency trade-offs across operating points. We release the datasets, parser adapters, and evaluation harness to support reproducible, field-level comparison of document parsing systems.

</details>


### [83] [DisPOSE: Projected Polystochastic Diffusion for Self-Supervised Multi-View 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07419)
*Tony Danjun Wang,Tolga Birdal,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisPOSE 是一个自监督框架，通过将多视角多人3D姿态估计中的离散分配问题建模为生成扩散过程，利用可微Sinkhorn投影和超图卷积解码器，在标准数据集和手术室等高遮挡场景中均取得SOTA性能，且对相机配置几乎无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法依赖合成3D姿态目录，在真实场景中因分布偏移而泛化能力差；多视角多人姿态估计中的人-视角分配问题本质上是离散的，缺乏有效的自监督解决方案。

Method: 1) 将多视角人员分配问题建模为多随机张量空间上的生成扩散过程；2) 在去噪过程中使用可微Sinkhorn投影，基于2D图像先验引导有效分配；3) 通过超图卷积解码器从多视角回归完整3D骨架，显式建模关节关系和结构。

Result: 在标准数据集上超越当前自监督SOTA方法；在新提出的高遮挡手术室基准上表现强劲；仅用10%伪标签即可保留99%性能，标签效率极高；对相机配置几乎无关。

Conclusion: DisPOSE 通过扩散过程与可微分配的巧妙结合，有效解决了多视角多人3D姿态估计中的离散分配问题，实现了高鲁棒性、高标签效率和对相机配置的强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human poses for multiple individuals from different camera views is a fundamental bottleneck for analyzing interacting behaviors. Existing self-supervised approaches leverage synthetic catalogues of 3D poses; however, this leads to poor generalization in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts. We therefore introduce DisPOSE, a self-supervised framework that approximates the inherently discrete multi-view person-assignment problem as a generative diffusion process over the space of polystochastic tensors. By employing differentiable Sinkhorn projections during denoising, our model learns to guide solutions toward valid and feasible assignments based on 2D image priors. The complete 3D skeletons of localized individuals are then regressed using a Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder that explicitly models relational structures and articulated joints across multiple views. The proposed approach outperforms current state-of-the-art self-supervised methods on standard datasets and demonstrates strong performance on a newly proposed benchmark featuring highly occluded scenes from surgical operating rooms. Our diffusion-based localization demonstrates high label efficiency, retaining 99% of its performance with only 10% of the pseudo-labels. Notably, disentangling the assignment and root regression components while maintaining differentiability makes DisPOSE nearly agnostic to different camera arrangements.

</details>


### [84] [OpenGlass: Open-Source Smart Glasses for On-Device Event-Based Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07431)
*Pietro Bonazzi,Julian Moosmann,Ahmet Celik,Philipp Mayer,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种开源智能眼镜平台，采用模块化设计支持事件相机和帧相机，通过硬件-软件协同电源管理系统实现低功耗，连续 ML 推理可达 11.8 小时，并演示了基于事件相机的 egocentric 手势识别管线，R(2+1)D 模型达到 83.94% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜受限于功耗、内存和计算能力，且缺乏支持事件相机和嵌入式 ML 的开源硬件平台，难以快速原型验证新型传感器和算法。

Method: 采用模块化 FPC 转接板设计，兼容事件相机和帧相机；硬件-软件协同电源管理（可配置 PMIC + nRF5340 事件驱动唤醒），GAP9 RISC-V SoC 在推理间隙断电；评估中使用极性分离事件直方图和 R(2+1)D 模型进行手势识别。

Result: 200 mAh 电池下实现 11.8 小时连续 ML 推理；egocentric 手势识别在 LynX 数据集上达到 83.94% 交叉主体准确率（macro F1=0.781），端到端延迟 33.9 ms；时间增强和去除模糊类别带来最大提升（+8.9 pp）。

Conclusion: 该开源平台解决了智能眼镜在小尺寸下的功耗和计算瓶颈，验证了事件相机与嵌入式 ML 的可行性，所有设计开源以促进社区研究。

Abstract: Smart eyewear enables unobtrusive, context-aware interaction through multimodal sensors and on-device intelligence, but is severely limited by power, memory, and compute constraints in a compact form factor. Open-hardware platforms supporting event-based vision and embedded ML at this scale are rare. This work introduces an open-source smart glasses platform for rapid prototyping of novel sensors and algorithms. Its modular design uses a flexible FPC interposer to support both event-based and frame-based cameras without full PCB redesign. A hardware-software co-designed power management system combines a configurable PMIC with event-driven wake-up via an nRF5340 coordinator, keeping the GAP9 RISC-V SoC powered down between inferences. The prototype achieves up to 11.8 hours of continuous on-device ML from a 200 mAh battery. As a demonstration, an egocentric hand gesture recognition pipeline was evaluated on the LynX dataset using polarity-separated event histograms from a Prophesee GENX320 camera. R(2+1)D achieved the best cross-subject accuracy of 83.94\% (macro F1 = 0.781) under leave-two-subjects-out validation, with 33.9 ms end-to-end latency on the GAP9. Temporal augmentation and removal of ambiguous classes provided the largest gains (+8.9 pp). All hardware designs, firmware, and models are released open source.

</details>


### [85] [The Lipreading Gap: Do VSR Models Perceive Visual Speech Like Human Lipreaders?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07435)
*Rishabh Jain,Naomi Harte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了视觉语音识别（VSR）系统与人类在唇读任务上的表现，发现尽管VSR模型在整体准确率上超越人类，但它们的成功与失败模式与人类不同，主要依赖训练数据中的语言线索而非视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前高性能的VSR模型是否真正具备了类人的视觉语音感知能力，还是仅仅在统计层面上表现更好。

Method: 在MaFI数据集上比较三种VSR系统与人类基线，使用词、字符、音素和视素级别的评估指标，并引入纯文本n-gram基线进行对比分析。

Result: VSR模型整体准确率更高，但在不同词汇上的成败模式与人类不同；模型错误更多受训练词频影响，而非视觉信息清晰度；在人类最难识别的视素上表现最好。

Conclusion: VSR系统主要依赖训练数据中的语言先验知识，而非真实的视觉感知能力，未能将视觉特征与有意义的词汇进行有效绑定。

Abstract: Visual speech recognition (VSR) models now surpass human lipreaders on benchmarks, but do such gains establish human-like visual speech perception? To explore this, we compare three VSR systems with human baselines on the MaFI word-level lipreading dataset using word, character, phoneme, and viseme-level metrics. Although models achieve higher overall accuracy, they succeed and fail on different words than humans. A text-only n-gram baseline given only a few initial phonemes rivals human lipreading. VSR word-level errors are consistently better explained by training word frequency than by the visual informativeness of words. Viseme accuracies, confusion matrices and human-model correlations further show that models gain most on visemes humans find hardest, and show much weaker dependence on visual clarity. Our work demonstrates that VSR systems rely primarily on language cues from training data rather than visual perception, failing to bind visual features into meaningful words.

</details>


### [86] [Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07436)
*Haoyuan Li,Zhengdong Hu,Jun Wang,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Skill-3D框架，通过场景记忆和技能库协同进化，使MLLM智能体在3D空间推理中学会根据场景自适应选择工具，显著提升工具使用正确率（从39%到78%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM智能体在3D空间推理中采用统一的工具使用策略，忽略了不同场景的异质性，导致工具滥用和偏好偏差，相比非智能体方法收益甚微。

Method: Skill-3D框架包含：(1) Scene Memory记录智能体工具使用轨迹；(2) 将相似场景的成功轨迹聚合蒸馏为场景感知技能，并附上失败教训；(3) 当相似场景再次出现时注入对应技能引导智能体；(4) 新轨迹的成功/失败进一步优化技能，形成记忆与技能库协同进化的闭环。

Result: VSI-Bench上工具使用正确率从39%提升至78%；MMSI-Bench上Gemini-3-Flash提升67%；技能引导轨迹后训练使Qwen3-VL-8B在VSI-Bench上提升43%。

Conclusion: 场景感知的技能学习能有效解决3D空间推理中工具使用策略的异质性问题，通过自我进化的闭环机制持续提升智能体的工具利用能力。

Abstract: This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 43% on VSI-Bench.

</details>


### [87] [Implicit Data Synthesis for Contrastive Unsupervised Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07498)
*Patrick Kage,Trevor Hedges,N. Siddharth,Pavlos Andreadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在科学观测数据中通过扰动网络权重而非数据本身来生成对比学习样本的方法，并在雷达流星观测数据集上使用SimCLR框架验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学观测数据标注成本高，传统对比学习依赖数据增强，但对科学数据而言，数据空间的扰动可能改变数据本质结构。

Method: 提出了通过扰动网络权重（而非输入数据）来生成对比样本的方法，并在SimCLR框架下进行实现。

Result: 在雷达流星观测数据上，该方法在匹配协议下表现出性能提升。

Conclusion: 权重扰动对比学习更适合科学观测数据，能更好地保留数据原始结构，提升无监督表示学习效果。

Abstract: Scientific observations generate large quantities of unlabeled data which is laborious to hand-label, making unsupervised learning techniques valuable for processing datasets. Among these approaches, contrastive learning provides a convenient mechanism for extracting structural representations from unannotated datasets. For natural imagery, the general approach is to use a variety of data-space augmentation methods in order to generate synthetic samples; however, for scientific observations data-space perturbations can fundamentally alter the underlying data. Our proposed method is to generate contrastive samples by perturbing the network weights rather than the underlying data, thus more closely preserving the structure of the data. We demonstrate this technique using a SimCLR-based pipeline applied over radar observations of meteors, and show performance gains under matched protocols.

</details>


### [88] [Differences in Detection: Explainability Where it Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07503)
*Johannes Theodoridis,Johannes Maucher,Andreas Schilling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DnD方法，通过匹配算法直接比较两个目标检测模型，揭示共享与差异错误，并可结合误差类型进行分析，为可解释性方法提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mAP和TIDE等标准指标只能独立评估每个模型，缺乏直接比较两个模型差异的能力，难以直观揭示模型间的共享错误和独有错误。

Method: 计算两个模型共同识别的GT标签交集，进而得到差异集和双方均遗漏的补集，并结合错误类型构建混淆矩阵进行分析。

Result: 能够直接、直观地比较两个检测模型，揭示个体和共享的错误模式，结合误差类型可自然分析检测误差的差异。

Conclusion: DnD不仅本身有价值，其最佳应用之一是引导ODAM等可解释性方法关注基于结构化子集的、与度量相关的示例。

Abstract: We propose Differences in Detection (DnD), an intuitive method to compare two object detection models. Based on the same matching algorithm, it complements the standard metrics of mean Average Precision ($mAP$) and TIDE error analysis with the ability to compare two models directly. More specifically, we calculate the intersection of ground truth labels that are recognized by both models, followed by the corresponding difference sets and the complement set of ground truth labels that are missed by both models. The resulting comparison is more direct and intuitive than a comparison of independent summary statistics. It reveals individual and shared mistakes and becomes particularly interesting when combined with error types. In this case, the differences in detection errors can be analyzed naturally in a standard confusion matrix. While valuable in itself, we believe that one of the best applications of DnD is to guide explainability methods such as ODAM towards metric-relevant examples, grounded in structured subsets. The code for our method is available here: https://github.com/JohannesTheo/differences-in-detection

</details>


### [89] [Streaming Video Generation with Streaming Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07508)
*Hanhui Wang,Yiming Xie,Haiwen Feng,Zhaoyang Lv,Shenlong Wang,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamForce是一个流式视频生成框架，通过连续力输入实现物理可控的视频生成，支持因果统一的局部和全局时变力控制，采用蒸馏管道实现力可控生成，在单GPU上可达16.6 FPS，在力遵循度和运动真实性方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在处理力控制时存在三个局限：不同力类型需训练不同模型、假设力是固定的、依赖非因果处理。作者希望构建一个因果统一模型，能够即时且连贯地响应局部和全局的时变力。

Method: 设计统一的力表示作为控制信号，开发用于力可控视频生成的蒸馏管道，结合自回归效率与力响应能力，采用因果处理方式支持连续力输入。

Result: 在单GPU上达到16.6 FPS的生成速度，在力遵循度和运动真实性两个指标上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: StreamForce证明了通过统一力表示和蒸馏管道可以实现高效、因果且物理可控的流式视频生成，为物理交互感知的视频生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce StreamForce, a streaming video generation framework that enables physically grounded control through continuous force inputs. Unlike prior video models that train separate models for different force types, assume fixed forces, or rely on non-causal processing, StreamForce is a causal and unified model that responds instantly and coherently to both local and global, time-varying forces. To achieve this, we design a unified force representation as a control signal and develop a distillation pipeline for force-controllable video generation. Our model combines autoregressive efficiency with force responsiveness, sustaining stable photometric and dynamic realism. StreamForce runs at up to 16.6 FPS on a single GPU, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both force adherence and motion realism. Project website: https://neu-vi.github.io/StreamForce/

</details>


### [90] [UniSHARP: Universal Sharp Monocular View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07514)
*Meixi Song,Dizhe Zhang,Hao Ren,Ruiyang Zhang,Bo Du,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSHARP，将SHARP扩展到通用单目渲染，通过统一的全向潜空间对齐，在特征和高斯空间中实现跨相机系统（从常规透视到全景）的神经渲染，并在构建的多系统基准测试中大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHARP只适用于针孔相机模型，无法处理广角鱼眼和全景相机。作者希望突破这一限制，实现跨不同相机系统的通用单目渲染。

Method: 核心思想是统一全向潜空间。具体做法：(1) 在特征空间和高斯空间中进行隐式对齐；(2) 高斯基元沿射线和径向距离排布，形成基于射线的通用表示；(3) 利用UniK3D启发的编码器提取2D语义和3D空间特征，联合解码生成完整高斯点云。

Result: 构建了涵盖多种成像系统（并按FoV分层）的基准，实验表明UniSHARP大幅优于替代方法。

Conclusion: UniSHARP有效解决了跨相机系统的通用单目渲染问题，为神经渲染在非针孔相机场景的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on extending SHARP, the popular photorealistic view synthesis method, for universal monocular rendering across a continuum of camera systems, from conventional perspective cameras to wide-field-of-view, fisheye and omnidirectional panoramic settings. To overcome the pinhole-specific assumptions of SHARP, our key idea is to align various images in a unified omnidirectional latent space. Thus, we propose UniSHARP, which performs implicit alignment in both feature and Gaussian spaces. Specifically, Gaussian primitives are arranged along rays and radial distances in a ray-based universal representation, while 2D semantic and 3D spatial features extracted from UniK3D-inspired encoders are jointly decoded to generate the complete Gaussian cloud. To comprehensively evaluate our method, we construct a benchmark covering diverse imaging systems across various scenes. The benchmark is further stratified by field of view (FoV) to enable fine-grained assessment of the universal monocular rendering task. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UniSHARP, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin. The project page can be found at: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Robots Need More than VLA and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06556)
*Elis Karcini,Faisal Mehrban,Quang Nguyen,Mac Schwager,Arash Ajoudani,Cesar Cadena,Jan Peters,Marco Hutter,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出机器人通用智能的瓶颈不仅是策略学习，更在于缺乏将非结构化行为数据转化为具身监督信号的机制，并提出四种关键接口组件作为未来研究重点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度聚焦于策略扩展（收集更多演示数据、训练更大VLA模型），忽略了如何利用人类运动、互联网视频、仿真数据等海量非结构化行为数据来训练机器人，这些数据缺乏具身动作标签、任务语义和奖励结构。

Method: 提出四个缺失的关键组件：1) 数据接口——自动标注非结构化行为；2) 具身接口——将人类运动重定向到机器人动作；3) 世界模型接口——基于物理的3D推理；4) 奖励接口——从视频和语言推断任务进展与成功。

Result: 调查了机器人基础模型、跨具身数据集、从视频学习、世界模型和奖励建模等领域的最新进展。

Conclusion: 提出未来研究应致力于构建能从更广泛物理世界（而非仅机器人演示）中学习的机器人系统。

Abstract: Generalist robot intelligence is often framed as a policy-scaling problem: collect more robot demonstrations, train larger Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and expect broader generalisation. In this position paper, we argue that this framing is incomplete. The central bottleneck is not only policy learning, but the absence of mechanisms that convert the world's abundant unstructured behavioural data into grounded robot supervision. Human motion, internet video, simulation rollouts, and interactive demonstrations contain rich information about tasks, goals, contacts, failures, and physical constraints, yet most of this information is not directly usable by robot policies because it lacks embodiment-specific action labels, task semantics, and reward structure. We identify four missing components for the next generation of robotics: data interfaces for autolabelling unstructured behaviour, embodiment interfaces for retargeting human motion to robot actions, world-model interfaces for physics-grounded 3D reasoning, and reward interfaces for inferring task progress and success from video and language. We survey recent progress in robot foundation models, cross-embodiment datasets, learning from video, world models, and reward modelling, and propose a research agenda for building robotics systems that can learn not only from robot demonstrations, but from the broader physical world.

</details>


### [92] [PhyRoGen: Synthetic Generation of Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzles Using Procedural Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06569)
*Lennart Julian Droß,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhyRoGen框架，利用程序化内容生成技术自动创建机器人操作拼图数据集，生成24种可解、可操作的物理拼图，为操作算法提供基准测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作物理拼图需要大量训练数据，而手动生成数据集耗时费力，因此需要自动化生成方法。

Method: 提出PhyRoGen框架，基于程序化内容生成（PCG）自动生成合成数据集，定义6个具体生成器，生成24种物理拼图，使用基于采样的规划算法求解。

Result: 所有拼图在1-300秒内可解，且在KUKA LBR iiwa机器人物理仿真中均可操作，验证了框架生成唯一、可解、可操作拼图的能力。

Conclusion: PhyRoGen能够自动生成独特的、可解的机器人操作拼图，为操作算法基准测试和鲁棒基础模型开发提供关键支撑。

Abstract: Robot manipulation of physical puzzles is important for automatic assembly and disassembly tasks. However, to enable robots to solve physical puzzles, manipulation skills need to be learned, which requires large training datasets, the generation of which is often time consuming and tedious. To overcome this problem, we propose the Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzle Generation framework (PhyRoGen), which leverages procedural content generation (PCG) for automated generation of synthetic datasets of manipulation puzzles. PhyRoGen is a general-purpose puzzle generator, which can generate physical puzzles with interlocking object dependencies, where one articulated object must be manipulated before another can be moved. Based upon PhyRoGen, we define six concrete generators which we use to generate 24 physical puzzles. By using a benchmarking framework, we are able to solve all puzzles in 1 to 300 seconds using sampling-based planning algorithms. Finally, we demonstrate that every generated puzzle is manipulatable by using a KUKA LBR iiwa robot in a physical simulation. This shows that our framework is able to procedurally generate unique, solvable robot manipulation puzzles, which is a crucial ingredient to benchmark manipulation algorithms and to develop robust foundation models.

</details>


### [93] [ChronoForest: Closed-Loop Multi-Tree Diffusion Planning for Efficient Bridge Search and Route Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06618)
*Jungmin Seo,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ChronoForest，一种结合局部桥梁搜索与在线路线重解的闭环规划系统，利用时间距离进行短程引导，同时在搜索时通过桥梁证据验证远程锚点连通性，在长距离路径规划任务中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线导航中，收集足够丰富的长程数据十分困难，而真实智能体仍需解决长距离任务并实现路线级效率，而非仅仅是可行性。微观上组合短程片段会产生搜索成本与路径质量间的权衡，宏观上航点排序需要比较起止点和航点锚点间的成对旅行成本，这些在规划前未知且仅凭长程时间距离估计会越来越不可靠。

Method: 提出ChronoForest闭环规划系统，包含锚链树扩散规划器（anchor-chaining tree diffusion planner）和在线多树编排器（online multi-tree orchestrator），耦合局部桥梁搜索与在线路线重解。利用时间距离进行短程引导和节点评估，同时通过搜索时桥梁证据验证远程锚点连通性，并反复重解路线。

Result: 在OGBench AntMaze-Stitch的中、大、巨型分拆上分别达到99.8%、99.3%和99.5%的成功率，在巨型拼接任务上相比先前基于扩散的结果提升最多34.5个百分点。在哈密顿路线组合基准上，在线重解纠正了不良时间排序并提高了路线质量，同时比穷举规划成本更低。

Conclusion: ChronoForest通过局部-全局结合的规划策略，有效解决了仅靠短程离线轨迹进行长距离路线规划的难题，在成功率和路径质量上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: How can we plan long-horizon routes that reach designated goals, visit required waypoints, and remain short when only short-horizon offline trajectories are available? This problem matters in offline navigation because collecting sufficiently rich long-horizon data is difficult, yet real agents must still solve long-range tasks with route-level efficiency rather than mere feasibility. The difficulty is twofold: at the microscopic level, composing many short-horizon segments creates a trade-off between search cost and path quality, while at the macroscopic level, waypoint ordering requires comparing pairwise travel costs among start, goal, and waypoint anchors that are unknown before planning and increasingly unreliable when estimated only from long-range temporal distance.
  In this paper, we propose ChronoForest, a closed-loop planning system that couples local bridge search and online route re-solving through an anchor-chaining tree diffusion planner and an online multi-tree orchestrator. ChronoForest uses temporal distance for short-range guidance and node evaluation, while using search-time bridge evidence to validate long-range anchor connectivity and repeatedly re-solve the route. On OGBench AntMaze-Stitch, ChronoForest achieves 99.8%, 99.3%, and 99.5% success on the medium, large, and giant splits and improves giant-stitch success by up to 34.5 points over prior reported diffusion-based results. On Hamiltonian route-composition benchmarks, online re-solving corrects poor temporal orderings and improves route quality while remaining substantially cheaper than exhaustive planning.

</details>


### [94] [What Matters When Cotraining Robot Manipulation Policies on Everyday Human Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06627)
*Richard Li,Aditya Prakash,Andrew Wen,Saurabh Gupta,Yilun Du,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用互联网日常视频（而非专门录制的演示视频）来辅助训练机器人操作策略，发现手部姿态质量影响迁移效果，但即使手部姿态准确，运动差异仍会阻碍迁移，除非视觉和策略网络针对不同形态进行专门化。提出的联合训练方法在低机器人数据场景下，六个操作任务的成功率平均提升了29.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略的联合训练主要依赖专门录制的演示视频（需要特殊硬件捕捉3D手部姿态），而互联网上存在大量日常视频但尚未被有效利用，因此需要探究哪些因素能促进从这类视频到机器人的知识迁移。

Method: 构建了一个包含532个日常人类视频、28小时高质量三角测量手部标注的新数据集，通过比较不同手部姿态质量、设计专用化视觉和策略网络来消除运动差异，并采用联合训练策略。

Result: 在低机器人数据场景下（六个操作任务），联合训练方法使绝对成功率提升了29.7%。

Conclusion: 手部姿态质量影响迁移效果，但仅靠准确手部姿态不够；视觉和策略网络需针对不同形态（人与机器人）进行专门化设计才能有效弥合运动差异，从而将互联网视频用于机器人操作策略训练。

Abstract: Human video datasets used for cotraining robot manipulation policies largely consist of curated demonstrations where motions are orchestrated to resemble robot behavior and 3D hand poses are captured with specialized hardware. A more plentiful source of data is everyday Internet video, but it is an open question what factors enable transfer from such videos to robots. We investigate this using a new dataset of 532 human videos with 28 hours of high-quality triangulated hand labels and natural motions. We find that hand pose quality affects transfer, but even with accurate hands, the inherent motion gap hinders transfer unless the vision and policy networks specialize to each embodiment. Our cotraining recipe yields consistent improvements, with an absolute success rate gain of $29.7\%$ in the low-robot-data regime across six manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [95] [On the Hardness of Optimal Motion on Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06686)
*Tzvika Geft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，证明了树结构上多智能体路径规划（MAPF）在距离、makespan和flowtime三种目标下，对于标记和2-着色两种变体均为NP难问题。特别地，解决了树结构上Pebble Motion问题几十年的复杂性开放问题，并首次给出彩色Pebble Motion的硬度结果。所有结果通过证明Stack Rearrangement问题的NP难性建立，揭示了多种基本运动模型的共同可处理性壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树结构上MAPF的多个基本变体的复杂性长期悬而未决，尤其是Pebble Motion问题在树上的复杂性已开放数十年；同时，Stack Rearrangement问题本身也是一个开放的优化问题。

Method: 通过将Stack Rearrangement问题归约到树上的MAPF问题（包括标记和2-着色变体），并证明Stack Rearrangement本身是NP难的，从而建立所有结果的硬度。利用栈结构连接简单树（细分星图）上的运动模型。

Result: 树上标记和2-着色MAPF在距离、makespan、flowtime三种目标下均为NP难；树上的Pebble Motion问题为NP难；2-彩色Pebble Motion在任何图类上首次获得硬度结果（紧的）；Stack Rearrangement问题被证明为NP难。

Conclusion: 揭示了树结构上多种基本运动模型存在共同的可处理性壁垒，统一并加强了先前的硬度结果；证明了即使是非常简单的树（细分星图）也已足够困难。

Abstract: This paper presents a simple framework that settles the complexity of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) on trees across standard objectives--distance, makespan, and flowtime--for both labeled and colored variants. In MAPF, agents occupy the vertices of a graph and must move to target vertices without collisions while optimizing a given objective. In the labeled case, the agents are distinct and have respective targets; in the colored case, agents of the same color are interchangeable. While many MAPF variants are known to be intractable, several basic cases on trees have remained open. We prove NP-hardness on trees for both labeled and 2-colored MAPF under all three objectives. In particular, we resolve the classical Pebble Motion problem, where one pebble moves at a time to an adjacent empty vertex and the goal is to minimize the total number of moves. Despite being one of the most basic discrete motion models, its complexity on trees had remained open for several decades. Moreover, for colored Pebble Motion, we give the first hardness result on any graph class, already with two colors, which is tight.
  All of these results are established through the hardness of Stack Rearrangement, itself posed as an open problem, which asks to optimally rearrange items stored in stacks, and which we also prove to be NP-hard. Notably, the connection to stacks yields hardness already on very simple trees--subdivided stars--across all problems. Together, these results reveal a common tractability barrier that permeates several fundamental motion models, thereby unifying and strengthening prior hardness results.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal Control Approach for Non-prehensile Ball Juggling Using a 7-DoF Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06704)
*Joel Ramadani,Vasilije Rakčević,Riddhiman Laha,Arne Sachtler,Valentin Le Mesle,Achim J. Lilienthal,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的控制框架，用于7自由度机械臂执行非抓取式抛球杂耍任务，通过两阶段最优控制方法生成稳定周期运动轨迹，并实现实时误差修正，在仿真和Franka Emika Panda机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取式物体操作（如杂耍）涉及高速动力学和混合系统，对控制输入的时序极其敏感，物体本身缺乏自校正能力，稳定性完全依赖于外力。现有方法难以系统性地解决这类高动态、欠驱动对象的稳定控制问题。

Method: 提出一个基于模型的控制框架：(1) 采用两阶段最优控制方法离线计算可行的杂耍运动模式；(2) 将离线轨迹组织起来，使得无需在线求解最优控制问题即可实现实时误差修正。

Result: 在仿真环境中评估了控制器的性能，并使用Franka Emika Panda机器人进行了真实实验，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型驱动框架能够系统性地生成稳定的杂耍轨迹，并实现实时误差校正，为高动态非抓取式操作任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Non-prehensile object manipulation skills are important for real-world robot interactions, enabling highly dynamic tasks such as balancing a glass on a tray or the controlled sliding of items on a table. Among such tasks, those characterised by high-speed manipulation requirements and general sensitivity of the resulting hybrid dynamics are particularly hard to accomplish. Within these, juggling can be seen as a highly challenging maneuver to be solved. The key to robotic juggling is achieving dynamic stabilisation of an underactuated object. Since the object does not possess the ability of self-correction, its stability is entirely dependent on the forces applied to it. This creates a system that is sensitive to control inputs, where timing is critical to continuously counteract deviations and maintain the desired behavior. We develop a systematic method to control a 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator performing non-prehensile ball juggling with a tool. Our primary contribution is a model-based framework for generating juggling trajectories and stabilizing a periodic juggling motion for this hybrid system. The framework incorporates a two-stage optimal control approach to compute the underlying feasible motion patterns required for stable juggling. Offline-computed trajectories are then organised to enable real-time error correction without solving optimal control problems online. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the resulting controller by first evaluating its performance in a simulation environment and performing an experiment using a Franka Emika Panda robot.

</details>


### [97] [SCOUT: Semantic scene COverage via Uncertainty-guided Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06721)
*Junyu Mao,Sara Ayoubi,Vishnu D. Sharma,Ilija Hadžić,Matthew Andrews*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT是一种在线语义探索框架，将主动遍历与概率场景图构建相结合，通过不确定性引导的遍历规划器平衡语义确定性增益、几何覆盖增益和移动成本，使机器人能够主动探索并逐步理解3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图方法将感知视为固定数据集的后处理阶段，场景表示与决定观察内容的决策脱钩，机器人无法在运行过程中主动理解场景。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架：在线构建不确定性感知的3D场景图（节点维护融合几何和开放词汇对象标签的后验信念，边编码结构关系），结合不确定性引导的遍历规划器（平衡语义确定性增益、几何覆盖增益和移动成本），使机器人主动选择视点。

Result: 系统将语义场景完整性作为操作目标而非语义建图的被动副产品，机器人可以在模糊对象上重新观察，并在场景不完整时扩展到未知自由空间。

Conclusion: 该方法推动了自主智能体在最少人工干预下巡逻、更新和推理不断变化的室内环境的能力。

Abstract: Robots that operate over extended periods should not merely visit space; they should progressively understand it. Yet most 3D scene graph pipelines treat perception as a post-processing stage over a fixed dataset, decoupling scene representation from the decisions that determine what is observed in the first place. We present SCOUT, an online semantic exploration framework that closes this loop by coupling active traversal with probabilistic scene graph construction. Given a prior 2D occupancy map and posed RGB-D observations, SCOUT incrementally builds an uncertainty-aware 3D scene graph whose nodes maintain fused geometry and posterior beliefs over open-vocabulary object labels, while edges encode structural relations such as on, inside, belong, and next to. These beliefs are fed back to an uncertainty-guided traversal planner, which selects viewpoints by balancing expected semantic certainty gain, geometric coverage gain, and travel cost. In this way, the robot revisits ambiguous objects when additional evidence matters and expands into unseen free space when the scene remains incomplete. The resulting system treats semantic scene completeness as an operational objective rather than a passive by-product of semantic mapping, moving toward autonomous agents that can patrol, update, and reason about evolving indoor environments with minimal human intervention.

</details>


### [98] [IDDMBSE: Integrating Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering for Trusted Autonomous Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06727)
*John S. Baras,Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,Clinton Enwerem,Praveen M. S. Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IDDMBSE，一种集成数据驱动与基于模型的系统工程方法，通过扩展MBSE V流程在每个步骤加入数据驱动循环，并基于SysML、自主系统栈和混合架构实现。配套开源工具链PERFECT、TRADES-X和VERITAS，在自主地面机器人上验证了整个开发生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主式信息物理系统处于MBSE与数据驱动ML/AI的交叉点，但缺乏原生覆盖两者的集成系统工程方法学。

Method: 提出IDDMBSE方法，在传统MBSE V流程的每一步扩展数据驱动循环；构建PERFECT（SysML转ROS自主栈）、TRADES-X（设计空间探索分解为模型优化+数据评估）、VERITAS（形式化+数据驱动+运行时验证）组成的开源工具链；在Isaac Sim仿真环境中进行全生命周期验证。

Result: 在自主地面机器人上验证了传感器选型、风险敏感路径规划、行为树任务验证、基于保形预测的鲁棒感知和多机器人协调等全生命周期应用，并在争议地形测试场完成测试。

Conclusion: IDDMBSE有效弥合了MBSE与数据驱动ML/AI之间的方法论鸿沟；未来方向是在SysML v2/KerML基础上实现语言原生可组合性和更紧密的ML/AI集成。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber-physical systems (CPS) sit at the intersection of Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) and data-driven Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence (ML/AI), yet no integrated Systems Engineering (SE) methodology natively spans both. We address this gap with IDDMBSE, an Integrated Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering methodology that extends the rigorous MBSE V-process with a data-driven loop at every step, anchored in SysML, the autonomy stack, and a hybrid model-based plus data-driven trade-off architecture. We instantiate IDDMBSE as an interoperable, open-source tool chain: PERFECT, which maps SysML system architectures to executable ROS autonomy stacks for scalable performance evaluation; TRADES-X, which decomposes design-space exploration into a model-based optimization stage followed by a data-driven evaluation stage; and VERITAS, which combines formal, data-driven, and runtime verification into a single assurance workflow. We demonstrate IDDMBSE on a Trusted Autonomous Ground Robot across its development lifecycle, spanning sensor-suite selection, risk-sensitive path planning, behavior-tree task verification, conformal-prediction-based robust perception, and assured multi-robot coordination, all exercised in a contested-terrain Isaac Sim test range that we release with the tool chain. We close by sketching how IDDMBSE is being re-formulated on SysML v2 / KerML foundations to enable language-native composability and tighter ML/AI integration.

</details>


### [99] [AxisGuide: Grounding Robot Action Coordinate System in RGB Observations for Robust Visuomotor Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06761)
*Jiyun Jang,Yujin Sung,Woosung Joung,Daewon Chae,Sangwon Lee,Sohwi Kim,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AxisGuide方法，通过渲染机器人基座坐标系的可视化线索通道来增强RGB观测，解决模仿学习策略在分布偏移下因缺乏动作坐标系理解而导致的性能下降问题。在LIBERO仿真和真实环境中验证了该方法能显著提升性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模行为克隆训练的视觉运动操作策略虽然具备强大的语义场景理解能力，但在分布偏移（如物体放置在新位置）下常常无法正确执行底层动作。作者认为这一差距源于对动作坐标系理解不足，即无法在图像空间中正确解释机器人的基座坐标系。

Method: AxisGuide利用相机参数和末端执行器位姿，在每个相机视图中渲染机器人基座坐标轴，并通过一组额外的线索通道增强RGB观测，显式地可视化+x、+y、+z方向运动在图像空间中的含义。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真环境和真实机器人环境中的大量实验表明，AxisGuide带来了显著的性能提升和更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式的动作坐标线索对于学习可靠且可迁移的通用视觉运动策略非常有效。

Abstract: Visuomotor manipulation policies trained via large-scale behavior cloning have achieved strong semantic scene understanding, yet often fail to reliably execute correct low-level actions under distribution shifts. For example, even in a simple pickup task with identical scene layouts, camera viewpoints, and illumination, performance can degrade substantially when the object is placed at unseen locations. We argue that this gap arises from insufficient action understanding, namely the inability to interpret the robot's base-frame action coordinate system in image space. To address this issue, we introduce AxisGuide, a lightweight guidance method that bridges semantic scene understanding and action-coordinate interpretation. Using camera parameters and end-effector poses, AxisGuide renders the robot base-frame axes in each camera view and augments RGB observations with a small set of cue channels that explicitly visualize the meaning of the +x, +y, and +z motions in image space. Extensive evaluations in both the LIBERO simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that AxisGuide yields substantial performance gains and improved generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit action-coordinate cues for learning reliable and transferable generalist visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-Robot Planning and Control from CCTV Camera Networks in a Real Warehouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06762)
*Luke Robinson,Benjamin Ramtoula,Anas Izaaryene,Paul Newman,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了首个仅依靠外部摄像头网络和边缘计算实现多机器人协同规划的方案，在真实仓库中用4台机器人和30个摄像头完成实验，无需机器人搭载任何导航硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将车载控制扩展到多机器人场景，利用环境中的摄像头网络实现大规模、低成本的机器人车队协调，同时避免复杂的标定和硬件依赖。

Method: 基于未标定的像素级拓扑相机图，采用分层规划器：上层为每个机器人选择相机序列，下层规划图像空间运动；协调策略采用优先先-联合规划，重叠相机区域视为一次仅一台机器人占有的共享资源，防止碰撞和死锁。

Result: 在真实仓库中部署4台机器人和30个摄像头（覆盖6条27米通道），报告了任务完成时间和协调统计数据，证明了方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个纯外部摄像头网络+离车计算实现多机器人规划与协调的现场演示，验证了将感知和计算完全从机器人端剥离的可行性。

Abstract: Off-board control of mobile robots from cameras embedded in the environment offers a practical path to scalable autonomy, moving sensing and compute off the robots. We extend this idea from the single-robot case to coordinated fleets in a real warehouse, driving multiple robots with only a distributed CCTV network and edge compute. The system operates entirely in image space over an uncalibrated, pixel-wise topological camera graph, enabling wide-area operation with flexible camera placement. A hierarchical planner selects a camera sequence per robot and plans its image-space motion through each view, coordinating robots with a prioritised-then-joint strategy and treating overlapping camera regions as shared resources held by one robot at a time to prevent collisions and deadlocks. We validate the approach in a real warehouse with four robots and 30 cameras across six 27 m aisles, reporting mission times and coordination statistics. To our knowledge, this is the first field demonstration of multi-robot planning and coordination using only an external camera network and off-board compute, with robots carrying no task-specific navigation hardware.

</details>


### [101] [Learning All-Terrain Locomotion for a Planetary Rover with Actively Articulated Suspension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06790)
*Arthur Bouton,Tristan D. Hasseler,Michael Paton,Travis Brown,Jacob Levy,William Reid,Joshua Martin,Hari Nayar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ERNEST是一种配备主动万向悬挂系统的四轮火星车，通过强化学习训练单一神经网络控制器，实现了在复杂松软地形上的自主障碍穿越，并成功完成从仿真到实物的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行星车的被动悬挂系统在松软地形和复杂障碍中受限，需要开发一种能主动重构车轮姿态、动态分配负载的悬挂系统，并实现单一控制器跨地形泛化，避免显式地形分类和控制器切换。

Method: 设计了两自由度主动万向悬挂（偏航+滚转）；使用DARTS高保真仿真引擎结合刚性接触动力学和Bekker-Wong土力学；通过强化学习训练单一神经网络控制器；采用策略整合（policy consolidation）将多地形专家策略合并为统一网络；通过域随机化、传感器噪声注入和系统辨识实现零样本迁移到实物。

Result: 自主穿越岩石区、凸起陷阱、轮高台阶、沙波纹和沙坡；在20°干沙坡上降低运输成本37%；在湿沙上主动悬挂表现优于完全无法移动的被动悬挂。

Conclusion: 主动万向悬挂结合强化学习可显著提升火星车在松软复杂地形上的自主越野能力，无需地形分类即可实现跨地形泛化，且能有效从仿真迁移到真实机器人。

Abstract: This paper presents ERNEST, a four-wheeled planetary rover concept equipped with a two-degree-of-freedom Active Gimbal Suspension that combines yaw and roll actuation to enable wheel reconfiguration, steering, and active load redistribution. A single neural network controller, trained to track a desired path across challenging terrain, fully unlocks the capabilities of this actuated suspension system for autonomous obstacle negotiation. A reinforcement learning framework is developed using the high-fidelity DARTS simulation engine, which combines rigid-contact dynamics and Bekker-Wong terramechanics, enabling the emergence of locomotion strategies adapted to loose-soil conditions. To obtain a single unified controller across heterogeneous terrains, a policy consolidation strategy merges the experience of terrain-specialized agents into one neural network, eliminating the need for explicit terrain classification and controller switching. The resulting controller operates on a combination of proprioceptive and exteroceptive feedback, including sparse stereo-derived terrain elevation, chassis attitude, joint states, and force-torque measurements. Zero-shot transfer to the physical rover is achieved through domain randomization, sensor noise injection, and model-to-real system identification. Experimental results demonstrate autonomous traversal of rock fields, a bump trap, a wheel-high step, sand ripples, and sandy slopes. On a 20° sandy slope, the learned controller reduces the cost of transport by 37% on dry sand despite the additional actuation, and achieves superior performance on wet sand where the passive suspension becomes completely immobilized.

</details>


### [102] [Lane Change Trajectory Planning for Personalized Driving Comfort and Mobility Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06805)
*Haoxuan Dong,Dongjun Li,Ziyou Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的换道轨迹规划器，结合三阶多项式轨迹生成与双头学习模块，通过门控切换机制实现个性化舒适性与机动性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换道过程中纵向与横向运动高度耦合且因人而异，现有方法难以同时兼顾个性化偏好与全工况可行性。

Method: 采用共享骨干网络+双头结构（一个保证全工况可行性，一个捕捉驾驶员个性偏好），通过基于误差胜者逻辑回归的统计门控机制自适应切换。

Result: 代表性案例与蒙特卡洛仿真表明，该方法在个性化数据充足时实现舒适与机动性优化，数据不足时基线头保证轨迹可行。

Conclusion: 所提方法能实现上下文感知的个性化换道轨迹规划，兼顾全工况安全与个体偏好。

Abstract: Lane changing entails simultaneous longitudinal and lateral motions that affect driving comfort and mobility efficiency. Because these motions are tightly coupled and subject to substantial inter-vehicle variability, trajectory planning for lane-change maneuvers is characterized by a highly personalized nature. This study proposes a neural network-driven planner that integrates a third-order polynomial trajectory generator with a learning module that infers optimal trajectory parameters across diverse driving conditions. Using a shared backbone with dual heads, one head ensures all-condition operational guarantees, while the other captures driver-specific preferences for comfort or mobility efficiency. A head-gated switching mechanism, realized through a statistical gate based on error-winner logistic regression, adaptively selects the appropriate head under varying driving conditions, which enables context-aware lane-change trajectory planning. Representative cases and Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed planner achieves personalized comfort and mobility during lane changes, while the baseline ensures feasible trajectories under driving conditions where personalized data are insufficient or inaccessible.

</details>


### [103] [Three-dimensional hydro-cluttered locomotion by an undulatory robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06829)
*Tianyu Wang,Matthew Fernandez,Galen Tunnicliffe,Nikolas Cornell,Justin Duong,Donoven Dortilus,Zhaochen J. Xu,Patricia Meza,Sean Lublinsky,Darsh Parikh,Jianfeng Lin,Emily Grace,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为AquaMILR的细长无肢机器人，通过可编程身体柔顺性、深度调节和惯性诱导滚动等机制，实现了在充满刚性/柔性障碍物的水下环境中高效、鲁棒的3D运动，并在真实红树林中验证了其导航与视觉检查能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境存在大量"水-障碍物混杂"空间，传统水中机器人难以应对不可避免的接触，缺乏在复杂障碍中可控运动的设计原理。

Method: 基于地面无肢机器人的机械智能原理，设计了AquaMILR机器人，采用双边线缆驱动、可编程身体柔顺性、分布式深度调节、防腐密封舱和机载电源，并通过系统性机器人物理实验验证。

Result: 可编程身体柔顺性可将身体-环境交互转化为快速稳健的前进；深度调节提供3D空间通行能力；惯性诱导的滚动可自动从卡住状态中恢复；在真实红树林中实现了导航和不可达根区的视觉检查。

Conclusion: 建立了水-障碍混杂环境中的运动原理，提出了一种将环境复杂性转化为运动资源的设计范式。

Abstract: Aquatic robots have expanded human access to underwater environments, yet many underwater spaces contain obstacles that can disrupt open-water locomotion. In "hydro-cluttered" environments, water is interspersed with rigid and flexible clutter, making body-obstacle contact unavoidable. Operating in these spaces requires robots that can regulate and exploit contact, but this regime remains difficult to model or simulate. Building on recent advances in mechanical intelligence in terradynamically capable limbless robotics, we develop principles for 3D aquatic locomotion using AquaMILR, an elongate limbless robot that combines bilateral cable-driven actuation, programmable body compliance, distributed depth regulation, corrosion-resistant enclosures, and onboard power and electronics for untethered field operation. Systematic robophysical experiments reveal that programmable body compliance regulates body deformation and converts body-environment interactions into fast, robust, forward progression across increasing hydro-clutter constraint strength. Depth regulation provides three-dimensional access, allowing the robot to bypass clutter, recover from obstruction, and continue through otherwise inaccessible routes. In potential jamming scenarios, emergent inertia-induced rolling acts as a spontaneous recovery mechanism, freeing the robot from clutter that would otherwise lead to failure and allowing locomotion to continue without additional control. Tests of the robot in an aquatic mangrove field demonstrate that these principles transfer to practical operation, enabling navigation and onboard visual inspection of inaccessible root zones. These results establish principles for hydro-cluttered locomotion and a design paradigm in which aquatic robots exploit environmental complexity as a locomotor resource.

</details>


### [104] [STRIPS-WM: Learning Grounded Propositional STRIPS-style World Models from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06832)
*Abhiroop Ajith,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出STRIPS-WM框架，从图像转换中学习STRIPS风格的符号世界模型，实现机器人长程视觉操作任务的经典规划，在视觉重排任务中优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行长程视觉操作任务时，观察高维图像但规划仅依赖与动作相关的事实。现有方法难以从原始视觉经验中自动获得具有前置条件和效果的符号化操作表示。

Method: 提出STRIPS-WM框架：1) 从图像转换中归纳有限抽象转换图；2) 学习潜在二元谓词和每个动作标签的一个接地命题操作符；3) 将所学谓词蒸馏到视觉编码器中，实现直接从新图像进行经典规划。

Result: 在视觉重排任务上，STRIPS-WM在图像到规划的成功率上优于视觉 rollout、潜在图搜索和潜在符号化基线方法。

Conclusion: 从视觉转换中学习符号化世界模型是可行的，且能有效提升长程视觉操作规划的成功率，弥合视觉感知与符号规划之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Robots performing long-horizon visual manipulation observe high-dimensional images, but successful plans depend on action-relevant facts: what can be done now and what changes afterward. A useful planning representation should discard irrelevant visual details while preserving action applicability and effects. Classical task planners exploit this structure through symbolic operators with preconditions and effects, but obtaining such representations from raw visual experience remains challenging. We study a visual task-planning setting in which a robot receives only image transitions: the current image, executed high-level action, and the resulting image. At test time, given a start image and a goal image, the robot must produce a sequence of high-level actions that reaches the goal. To address this problem, we introduce STRIPS-WM, a framework for learning image-grounded STRIPS-style world models directly from visual transitions. STRIPS-WM first induces a finite abstract transition graph from images, then learns latent binary predicates and one grounded propositional operator per action label. The learned operators form a symbolic action model with sparse preconditions and add/delete effects. Finally, the learned predicates are distilled into a visual encoder, enabling classical planning directly from novel start and goal images. Experiments on visual rearrangement tasks show that STRIPS-WM improves image-to-plan success over the tested visual rollout, latent graph-search and latent-symbolic baselines.

</details>


### [105] [Think Like a Pilot: Fine-Grained Long-Horizon UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06836)
*Xiangyi Zheng,Xiangyu Wang,Qinan Liao,Zimu Tang,Yue Liao,Dongyue Lyu,Guodong Wang,Junjie Liu,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了FLIGHT基准和FLIGHT VLA架构，用于解决无人机在长时语义指令下实现连续飞行动作控制的问题。FLIGHT VLA通过异步架构将低频任务推理VLM与高频扩散动作模型解耦，在包含细粒度VLN和长时Flow两个子集的数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准使用离散或粗粒度动作，而无人机VLA任务仅聚焦短时原子动作，缺乏支持长时连续飞行指令的无人机任务设定。

Method: 1) 构建FLIGHT基准，包含细粒度VLN和长时Flow两个数据集，提供多阶段指令与密集6-DoF轨迹标注。2) 提出FLIGHT VLA异步架构，将低频Streaming Pilot VLM（负责任务状态推理）与高频扩散动作模型（负责连续控制）解耦，并由显式Pilot Reasoning文本监督。

Result: 在闭环评估中，FLIGHT VLA在FLIGHT基准上持续超越代表性的VLN和VLA基线方法，在多阶段任务完成、子目标遵循和终端控制方面表现更强。

Conclusion: FLIGHT VLA架构有效，其训练的Streaming Pilot Reasoning VLM进一步提升了无人机视频推理能力。

Abstract: Language-guided UAV agents must execute long-horizon semantic instructions while producing smooth, physically feasible continuous flight commands, yet existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks typically use discrete or coarse actions and existing UAV Vision-Language-Action (VLA) tasks focus on short, atomic maneuvers. To address this gap in UAV task settings, we introduce \textbf{FLIGHT}, a \textbf{F}ine-grained \textbf{L}ong-horizon \textbf{I}nstruction-\textbf{G}uided benchmark for \textbf{H}ybrid UAV navigation and reasoning \textbf{T}asks, which combines multi-stage instructions with dense 6-DoF trajectory annotations across two dataset splits: Fine-grained VLN and Long-horizon Flow. To endow the UAV agent with the capability of real-time in-flight reasoning over task execution status and mission planning, while simultaneously accommodating high-frequency, real-time precise control, we further propose \textbf{FLIGHT VLA}, an asynchronous architecture that decouples a low-frequency Streaming Pilot Vision-Language Model (VLM) for task-state reasoning from a high-frequency diffusion action model for continuous control, supervised by explicit \textbf{Pilot Reasoning} texts that summarize the current flight state and anticipate the next subgoal. In closed-loop evaluation, FLIGHT VLA consistently surpasses representative VLN and VLA baselines on our FLIGHT benchmarks, achieving stronger multi-stage completion, subgoal adherence, and terminal control. Its trained Streaming Pilot Reasoning VLM further improves UAV video reasoning, validating the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [106] [What Is My Robot Thinking? Design Considerations for Transparent and Trustworthy Shared Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06870)
*Atharv Belsare,Zohre Karimi,Connor Mattson,Rushiil Nakka,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究共享自主系统中的透明度设计（视觉/听觉反馈、信息丰富度）对用户协调和信任的影响，发现提高目标可读性比完全披露更能改善协调，而信任依赖于任务适配的信息暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享自主系统中机器人内部意图推理不可直接观测，导致推断意图与用户真实意图不匹配会破坏协调与信任，因此需要探究透明度设计如何改善人机交互。

Method: 在基于视觉的共享自主系统中，设计视觉vs听觉反馈和稀疏vs丰富信息两种透明度维度，通过N=25名用户的两项辅助操作任务实验，评估协调效果和信任水平。

Result: 提供反馈显著改善了意图对齐并减少纠正性干预；用户偏好视觉反馈而非听觉反馈；信息丰富度的偏好取决于任务复杂度；揭示完整信念分布并未持续改善对齐或信任。

Conclusion: 有效的透明度主要通过提高目标可读性来增强协调，而信任取决于任务适配的信息暴露程度，而非最大程度的信息披露。

Abstract: Assistive robots operating under shared autonomy must balance user control with autonomous assistance. Because robot actions depend on internal intent inference that is not directly observable, mismatches between inferred and intended goals can undermine coordination and trust. We investigate how interface-level transparency, including feedback modality (visual vs. auditory) and information richness (sparse vs. rich), shapes interaction in a vision-based shared autonomy system. In a user study with N=25 participants across two assistive manipulation tasks, we evaluate how these designs influence coordination and trust. Providing feedback significantly improves intent alignment and reduces corrective intervention, indicating that making the inferred goal legible accelerates convergence in shared control. Participants preferred visual over auditory feedback, while preferences for sparse versus rich information depended on task complexity. We also found that revealing the full belief distribution did not consistently improve alignment or trust. Together, these findings indicate that effective transparency enhances coordination primarily through goal legibility, while trust depends on task-appropriate information exposure rather than maximal disclosure. Based on these results, we outline guidelines for designing transparent shared autonomy systems.

</details>


### [107] [Neuro-Symbolic Learning for Long-Horizon Task Planning Under Complex Logical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06877)
*Qiwei Du,Zitong Zhan,Shaoshu Su,Bowen Li,Yi Du,Zhipeng Zhao,Taimeng Fu,Sebastian Scherer,Jiaoyang Li,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对神经符号任务规划中的暴露偏差问题，提出基于命令学习的双层优化框架，通过并行修复、重启、回滚（3R）策略稳定训练过程，在多个基准上实现SOTA性能（失败率降低80.04%，规划时间减少57.14%），并在四足移动机械臂上完成仿真与真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法在离线监督下学习物体重要性评分，但部署时规划器在模型自身不完美预测产生的剪枝空间中运行，导致训练-测试不匹配（暴露偏差），严重降低规划性能。本文旨在解决这一关键瓶颈。

Method: 将物体重要性学习建模为基于命令学习的双层优化问题：上层优化神经评分器，下层在评分剪枝后的搜索空间内求解符号规划问题；引入3R策略（并行修复、重启、回滚）为上层学习提供稳定可靠的自适应反馈。

Result: 三个挑战性基准上实现SOTA：失败率降低80.04%，规划时间减少57.14%。在四足移动机械臂的仿真与真实环境中进一步验证了框架的可部署性。

Conclusion: 提出的双层优化框架结合3R策略有效解决了暴露偏差问题，显著提升了长时序任务规划的效率与鲁棒性，为可部署的神经符号任务规划提供了新方案。

Abstract: Task planning often suffers from severe efficiency bottlenecks when robots must reason over long-horizon action sequences under complex logical constraints, including object affordances, spatial relationships, and sequential action dependencies. Recent neuro-symbolic methods improve planning efficiency by learning object-importance scores to prune task-irrelevant objects, but they typically rely on fixed offline supervision generated from full search spaces. This creates a train-test mismatch: at deployment, the planner operates in pruned search spaces induced by the model's own imperfect predictions, leading to exposure bias and degraded planning performance. To address this challenge, we formulate object-importance learning for task planning as an imperative learning-based bilevel optimization problem. The upper level optimizes a neural scorer, while the lower level solves a symbolic planning problem in the score-pruned search space. To stabilize this learning process, we introduce a 3R strategy into the lower-level planning, using parallel Repair, Restart, and Rollback recovery to provide reliable and adaptive feedback for upper-level learning. Experiments on three challenging benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, including an 80.04% reduction in failure rate and a 57.14% reduction in planning time. We further validate the framework on a quadruped-based mobile manipulator in simulation and the real world, demonstrating its potential for efficient and deployable neuro-symbolic task planning.

</details>


### [108] [A Cross-view Fusion Framework for Robust 6-DoF Grasp Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06878)
*Kangjian Zhu,Haobo Jiang,Jianjun Qian,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨视图融合框架，通过辅助视角和后融合策略增强角落视角下6-DoF抓取姿态估计的鲁棒性，并引入自监督对比学习与圆柱对齐集成模块，在GraspNet-1Billion基准和真实场景中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角落视角下6-DoF抓取姿态估计存在严重遮挡问题，且传统多视图重建方法耗时且与抓取任务无关，亟需一种高效、鲁棒的跨视图融合方案。

Method: 提出跨视图融合框架，包含三部分：1）自监督对比学习策略，利用跨视图点对匹配/非匹配关系正则化点云特征；2）跨视图对齐的圆柱体集成模块，通过相似性对齐、圆柱坐标系注册及局部自注意力与种子交叉注意力交替层，融合抓取相关几何信息。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion基准测试和真实机器人抓取应用中均取得强性能表现。

Conclusion: 提出的跨视图融合框架有效解决了角落视图下的遮挡问题，通过对比学习和圆柱对齐集成模块显著提升了抓取估计的鲁棒性和准确率，代码已开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a cross-view fusion framework that enhances the robustness of 6-DoF grasp pose estimation in corner views. Our framework alleviates occlusion by incorporating an auxiliary view and avoids the time-consuming, task-agnostic multi-view reconstruction through a post-fusion strategy. To enhance cross-view fusion, we propose a self-supervised contrastive learning strategy that leverages cross-view associations to regularize point cloud features. In brief, a cross-view point pair is considered a match if the two points correspond to the same 3D location, and a non-match if they represent distinct grasp directions. The learning strategy significantly enhances the spatial consistency and direction distinctiveness of point features, thereby facilitating cross-view fusion and improving estimation robustness. Furthermore, we propose a cross-view-aligned cylinder integration module to fuse grasp-relevant geometry into a comprehensive representation. Specifically, the module first aligns the cross-view points and features according to their similarity to enhance the robustness against noise. Subsequently, these points are registered into the cylindrical coordinate frame, emphasizing the rotation-symmetric geometry which is important for grasping. Finally, local self-attention and seed cross-attention layers are alternately employed, respectively enabling interactions within single views and across views, which supports fine-grained representation of grasp-relevant geometry. Our framework achieves strong performance on the GraspNet-1Billion benchmark and in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/KJZhuAutomatic/Cross-view-Grasp.

</details>


### [109] [ActionMap: Robot Policy Learning via Voxel Action Heatmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06904)
*Pei Yang,Hai Ci,Yanzhe Chen,Qi Lv,Han Cai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActionMap，一种替代VLA模型中原有动作解码器的体素热图动作头，通过将动作空间结构化为体素网格并预测热图，在LIBERO仿真和真实Franka操作任务中，相比现有方法（如OpenVLA-OFT的L1回归头）实现显著性能提升（平均+8.2%），且训练更高效、数据需求更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型的动作解码器（单点预测器）将动作空间视为无结构空间，未能利用相邻动作的几何邻近性，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出ActionMap，一种可替换现有VLA动作解码器的体素热图动作头。对每个新动作，在动作空间上预测体素热图，每个体素存储对应动作的概率。

Result: 在LIBERO四套任务平均上比OpenVLA-OFT的L1回归头提升8.2%，在两个不同架构骨干上收敛速度相当或更快，且低数据量下数据效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 动作表示是提升VLA性能的真正杠杆，区别于进一步扩大骨干网络或训练策略；ActionMap可作为跨骨干网络的通用改进方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly across backbones, training recipes, and data scale, yet the action decoder, which converts the backbone's hidden state into a continuous control signal, has barely changed and remains a single-point predictor across the majority of current VLAs. Whether implemented via autoregressive token bins, L1 regression, or flow-matching denoising, the resulting decoder treats the action space as unstructured, leaving the geometric proximity of neighboring actions unexploited during training. To advance this, we introduce ActionMap, a voxel heatmap action head that drops into an existing VLA in place of its native action decoder. For each new action, the head predicts a voxel heatmap over the action space, where each voxel directly stores the probability of the corresponding action. Across LIBERO simulation and real-world Franka manipulation, our heatmap head surpasses two architecturally distinct backbones at matched training steps (e.g., +8.2% over OpenVLA-OFT's L1 regression head on the LIBERO four-suite average), converges at comparable or faster rates on both backbones, and remains markedly more data-efficient at low training data. The cross-backbone consistency indicates that action representation is a real lever for VLA performance, distinct from further backbone or recipe scaling. Project Page: https://github.com/showlab/ActionMap.

</details>


### [110] [T-GMP: Terrain-conditioned Generative Motion Priors for Versatile and Natural Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06944)
*Junhong Guo,Hao Hu,Chen Chen,Haoxuan Han,Linao Gong,Xin Yang,Zhicheng He,Yao Su,Fenghua He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出T-GMP模块，利用CVAE从少量专家演示中学习地形条件化的运动先验，并结合对抗学习与立足点惩罚机制，实现自然且鲁棒的双足机器人地形适应运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法依赖固定的运动先验，难以适应多变地形，无法同时兼顾类人自然性和复杂地形的鲁棒穿越。

Method: 1) 使用CVAE从少量状态-地形专家演示中学习地形条件化的潜在运动流形；2) 将T-GMP集成到对抗学习框架中；3) 提出Foothold Penalty（立足点惩罚），让判别器根据地形的局部特征动态调节自然性约束。

Result: 在穿越成功率、运动平滑度上优于现有基线，同时保持了仿生自然且物理协调的运动。

Conclusion: T-GMP通过地形条件化的运动先验和动态约束机制，有效统一了类人自然性与地形适应性，为双足机器人的通用运动控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Achieving both anthropomorphic naturalness and robust terrain traversal remains a fundamental challenge in humanoid locomotion. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches typically rely on fixed motion priors, limiting their adaptability to varying environments. We propose Terrain-conditioned Generative Motion Priors (T-GMP), a module that captures a terrain-conditioned latent motion manifold from a few expert state-terrain demonstrations using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE). The learned priors enable smooth style transitions, facilitating a unified policy that adapts to terrain variations. We integrate T-GMP into an adversarial learning pipeline with our proposed Foothold Penalty, where a discriminator dynamically modulates naturalness constraints conditioned on local terrain features, guiding the generation of versatile and human-like motions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in traversal success rate and motion smoothness, while preserving biomimetically natural and physically coordinated motions.

</details>


### [111] [LIMMT: Less is More for Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06953)
*Yu Guan,Zekun Qi,Chenghuai Lin,Xuchuan Chen,Dairu Liu,Wenyao Zhang,Jilong Wang,Xinqiang Yu,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMMT提出数据质量比数据量更重要，仅用AMASS数据集3%的数据即可达到更优的运动跟踪效果，通过物理可行性、多样性和复杂性三个维度定义运动数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的运动数据可以在训练早期引导跟踪策略朝着更优的优化轨迹发展，而以往研究忽视了数据质量的重要性。

Method: 提出LIMMT框架，从物理可行性、多样性和复杂性三个维度定义运动数据质量，对AMASS和网络来源的动捕数据进行清洗筛选。

Result: 仅用AMASS 3%以下的数据即可达到比全量数据训练更好的跟踪性能，并在网络来源动捕数据上也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 运动数据质量比数据量更为关键，LIMMT是首个针对物理仿真人形运动跟踪的数据中心研究。

Abstract: We argue that high-quality motion data can steer tracking policies toward better optimization trajectories early in training. In this work, we introduce LIMMT (Less Is More for Motion Tracking). To our knowledge, this is the first data-centric study for physics-based humanoid motion tracking. We go beyond simply removing low-quality and erroneous clips, but define motion data quality through three dimensions: physics feasibility, diversity, and complexity. We show that even training with under 3% of AMASS yields better tracking performance than training with the full dataset. We further conduct data cleaning on the estimated web-sourced mocap data. Extensive experiments and analyses validate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [112] [Compliance-Based Sensor Placement for Force Sensing on a Sensorized Prostate Phantom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06977)
*Sizhe Tian,Yinoussa Adagolodjo,Jeremie Dequidt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于柔顺性的传感器放置方法，用于前列腺数字直肠检查训练仿真体模的力传感。通过加权贪心选择策略，优先重建临床相关的后部接触区域力信号，相比全局QR策略，目标区域重建得分平均提升22.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在软体机器人医疗仿真体模中实现有限且实用的传感器配置下，提升力的可观测性，特别是临床相关的后部接触区域。

Method: 构建有限元仿真数据集，建立力输入与压力/位移响应之间的柔顺矩阵；提出加权贪心选择策略，在最大化局部力可重建性的同时，优先考虑临床感兴趣的后部接触区，并避免在感兴趣区域内直接放置标记点。

Result: 与全局QR放置策略相比，所提方法在目标区域的平均可重建得分提高了22.5%。

Conclusion: 区域感知的稀疏传感器放置方法能够在保持有限且实用传感配置的同时，有效提升软体医疗仿真体模中的力观测能力。

Abstract: This work presents a compliance-based sensor placement method for force sensing on a sensorized prostate phantom designed for Digital Rectal Examination training. The phantom combines three internal pneumatic chambers, used as intrinsic pressure sensors, with ten surface displacement markers. A finite-element simulation dataset is generated by applying external forces at sampled surface locations, from which a compliance matrix relating force inputs to pressure and displacement responses is constructed. Based on this matrix, we propose a weighted greedy selection strategy that maximizes local force reconstructability while prioritizing the clinically relevant posterior contact region and avoiding marker placement directly within the Region of Interest. Compared with a global QR-based placement strategy, the proposed method increases the mean reconstructability score in the target region by 22.5%.
