These results suggest that region-aware sparse sensor placement can improve force observability in soft robotic medical phantoms while maintaining a limited and practical sensing configuration.

</details>


### [113] [Mission-Level Runtime Assurance Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06996)
*Chieh Tsai,Salim Hariri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶的运行时安全框架，在传统车辆安全基础上引入任务级安全监控，能够检测并拒绝导致任务不可行的驾驶指令，实验证明该方法优于仅关注平台级安全的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶运行时安全方法主要关注即时的车辆安全性（如碰撞避免），但忽视了高层驾驶指令可能导致的**任务级故障**（如跳过检查点、进入禁行区域、生成无法完成任务的未来路线），因此需要一种同时评估车辆安全和任务完成能力的框架。

Method: 1. 扩展highway-env环境，引入任务级故障场景（跳过检查点、进入禁行区、生成不可行未来路线）；2. 设计运行时监控系统，在执行前检测并拒绝对任务不安全或不可行的指令；3. 基于Simplex-Drive框架实现基线方法（学习型驾驶控制+安全回退控制+运行时控制器切换）用于对比。

Result: 仅依赖平台级运行时安全无法检测任务级规划故障；所提框架能够成功拒绝任务不可行指令，在随机故障条件下显著提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶安全不仅需要保证车辆即时安全，还需要在运行时监控任务级的可行性，所提框架填补了这一空白并验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime safety for autonomous driving when high-level driving commands become faulty or unreliable. Unlike conventional runtime-safety approaches that mainly focus on immediate vehicle safety, the proposed framework evaluates both driving safety and whether the vehicle can still successfully complete its mission before a command is executed. The framework extends highway-env with mission-level fault scenarios such as skipping required checkpoints, entering restricted areas, and generating future routes that can no longer complete the mission successfully. A runtime monitoring system is introduced to detect and reject unsafe or mission-infeasible commands before execution. For comparison, an adapted Simplex-Drive runtime-safety baseline with learning-based driving control, safety fallback control, and runtime controller switching is implemented using the public Simplex-Drive framework. Experimental results show that platform-level runtime safety alone cannot detect mission-level planning faults, while the proposed framework successfully rejects mission-infeasible commands and improves mission success under randomized fault conditions.

</details>


### [114] [Task Editing for Generalizable 3D Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07012)
*Jian-Jian Jiang,YiHan Yang,Lan Wei,Yuming Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Xuhang Chen,Bin Fan,Dandan Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Task-Edit是一个从任务中心编辑视角出发的演示生成框架，通过将任务解耦为场景、技能和物体三个组件并灵活重组，实现多样化的轨迹生成，显著提升3D视觉运动策略在长程操作任务中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉运动策略依赖大规模真实世界演示数据，成本高昂；现有的演示生成策略（如物体中心变换）仅能做局部变化，难以合成多样化的场景-技能-物体组合，尤其对复杂长程任务效果有限。

Method: 提出Task-Edit框架，将任务分解为场景（scene）、技能（skill）和物体（object）三个独立组件，并支持灵活重组这些组件，从而生成多样化的轨迹数据，实现可扩展的演示生成。

Result: 在真实世界实验中展示了三个优势：(1)有效性：显著提升多种任务和机器人形态的3D视觉运动策略；(2)泛化性：提高模型在不同场景下的泛化能力；(3)适用性：使模型能够处理抗干扰、避障和未见过的杂乱场景等难以采集的真实场景。

Conclusion: Task-Edit通过任务解耦与重组的方式，有效解决了3D视觉运动策略的数据效率瓶颈，为长程操作任务提供了可扩展的演示生成方案。

Abstract: 3D visuomotor policies offer a promising direction for complex robotic manipulation, as depth maps and point clouds provide rich geometric information for spatial reasoning. However, their success often depends on large-scale real-world demonstrations, which are costly and time-consuming to collect. To this end, existing methods commonly use demonstration generation strategies to improve data efficiency by applying object-centric transformations to human-collected demonstrations, such as varying object poses or scales. While effective for local variation, these transformations largely preserve the original scene structure and skill sequence, limiting their ability to synthesize diverse scene-skill-object combinations for complex tasks. In this paper, we propose Task-Edit, a novel demonstration generation framework that generates diverse trajectories from a task-centric editing perspective. The key insight of Task-Edit is to decompose a task into scene, skill and object components, and flexibly recombine them. In this way, Task-Edit enables scalable demonstration generation and significantly improves generalization for long-horizon manipulation tasks. We evaluate Task-Edit through extensive real-world experiments and demonstrate three advantages: (1) Effectiveness: Task-Edit significantly improves 3D visuomotor policies across various real-world tasks and robot embodiments. (2) Generalizability: Task-Edit improves model generalization across different scenario setups. (3) Applicability: Task-Edit enables models to handle scenarios that are difficult to collect in the real world, including disturbance resistance, obstacle avoidance and unseen cluttered scenes.

</details>


### [115] [A Multi-Operator Mixed-Reality Interface for Multi-Robot Control and Coordination: Co-Located and Private Workspace Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07013)
*Omotoye Shamsudeen Adekoya,Antonio Sgorbissa,Carmine Tommaso Recchiuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合现实界面HORUS，支持多操作员协作控制机器人团队，包含共享工作区与私有工作区两种模式。实验表明共享工作区虽在任务效率上与私有模式相当，但显著提升了协作感知、共享理解与交接清晰度，是更受偏好的协作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多操作员控制机器人团队需要共享态势感知并防止指令冲突，现有系统多限于单操作员场景，缺乏有效的多操作员协作机制。

Method: 基于HORUS界面扩展为多操作员协作系统，包含共置共享工作区和独立私有工作区两种模式；架构结合注册驱动的场景构建、轻量级共享会话同步和每机器人控制租约机制来协调任务分配并避免冲突。

Result: 36名参与者（18对）控制三台Nova Carter机器人的实验表明：两种模式的任务客观性能相当，但共享工作区在感知协作、共享理解和交接清晰度上显著更优，且是用户偏好的协作模式。

Conclusion: 物理共置混合现实工作空间能有效改善操作员之间的协调性，即使底层机器人控制工具不变，协作体验仍获提升。

Abstract: Multi-operator control of robot teams requires not only access to the same mission information, but also mechanisms for maintaining shared awareness and preventing conflicting interventions. Building on our previous HORUS interface (Holistic Operational Reality for Unified Systems) we present a mixed-reality interface that extends single-operator multi-robot supervision to collaborative multi-operator use. The system supports two complementary modes: a co-located shared workspace, in which operators observe and manipulate the same mini-map in the same physical location, and a private-workspace mode, in which operators work on the same mission through independently placed local workspaces. The architecture combines registration-driven scene construction, lightweight shared-session synchronization, and per-robot control leases to support collaborative monitoring, tasking, and teleoperation while preventing conflicting commands. We evaluated the approach in a human-subject study with 36 participants (18 pairs) controlling three Nova Carter mobile robots in two search environments. The performance of the objective task was comparable across the two modes, indicating that both modes supported effective mission execution. However, the co-located shared workspace significantly improved perceived collaboration, shared understanding, and handoff clarity, and was the preferred collaborative mode. These results indicate that physically co-locating the MR workspace improves how operators coordinate even when the underlying robot-control tools remain unchanged.

</details>


### [116] [Extending Responsibility-Sensitive Safety for the Assessment of Offloaded Autonomous Driving Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07067)
*Robin Dehler,Aryan Thakur,Michael Buchholz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶中功能卸载带来的安全问题，扩展了RSS安全模型以考虑不同响应时间，提出基于安全约束的卸载决策与回退机制，并通过热备份策略提升安全性。实验证明该方法在保证分布式计算优势的同时提升了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖功能卸载提升计算效率与能耗，但卸载后的服务组合因V2X通信导致响应时间增加且不稳定，直接影响车辆反应时间与安全保证。现有方法未充分解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 扩展RSS定义，显式区分本地与卸载服务组合的不同响应时间；2) 将RSS安全约束融入功能卸载决策，仅当当前交通状况在对应端到端响应时间下仍安全时才允许卸载；3) 设计受控回退机制，当安全条件被违反时回退到本地执行；4) 提出含热备阶段的增强回退策略，实现更快速安全的过渡。

Result: 通过仿真和真实世界实验，所提方法相比现有功能卸载与安全框架，在保证分布式计算优势的同时，显著提升了安全性。

Conclusion: 将安全约束显式融入功能卸载决策是可行的，热备份回退策略能有效缓解卸载带来的安全风险，为自动驾驶中安全与效率的平衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Safety is a fundamental requirement in the development of autonomous driving (AD) systems. While function offloading has demonstrated significant benefits in terms of computational efficiency and energy consumption, its application to safety-critical AD functionality introduces new challenges. In particular, offloaded service compositions incur increased and variable response times due to wireless vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, which directly affects the vehicle's reaction time and thus its safety guarantees. In this paper, we address this challenge by extending the definitions of Responsibility-Sensitive Safety (RSS) to explicitly account for different response times of local and offloaded AD service compositions. Based on this extension, we propose an integration into function offloading, using the RSS safety constraints for offloading decision-making and fallback mechanisms. Offloaded service compositions are only permitted if the current traffic situation remains safe under the corresponding end-to-end response time. If this condition is violated, the system performs a controlled fallback to local execution. Furthermore, we introduce an enhanced fallback strategy that includes a warm-standby phase for offloaded services, enabling faster and safer transitions from offloaded to local services. The proposed approach is integrated into our AD stack and evaluated in both simulation and the real world. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves safety compared to state-of-the-art function offloading and safety frameworks, while preserving the benefits of distributed computation when safety conditions allow.

</details>


### [117] [Predictive Style Matching: Natural and Robust Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07083)
*Simeon Nedelchev,Ekaterina Chaikovskaia,Egor Davydenko,Eduard Zaliaev,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测式风格匹配（PSM）方法，通过离线预测器将机器人下半身状态映射为上半身关节和步态目标，在不牺牲抗干扰能力的前提下大幅提升人形机器人运动风格质量，相比任务奖励方法和运动模仿方法取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：任务奖励方法产生僵硬不对称步态，运动模仿方法虽提升外观但对外部干扰更敏感，因为参考信号可能对抗恢复平衡所需的瞬态姿态。

Method: 提出PSM，训练阶段使用离线预测器根据机器人下半身状态历史和速度指令生成可解释的上半身关节与步态目标来塑造奖励；预测器是状态条件而非时间索引，且仅在训练时使用，部署时保持与纯任务RL相同的本体感知接口和推理成本。

Result: 在Unitree G1上（仿真和硬件），PSM相比纯任务RL将上半身风格误差降低约一个数量级，同时保持摔倒恢复率；而运动模仿基线虽风格误差最低，但摔倒恢复失败率高出约5倍。

Conclusion: PSM有效平衡了运动风格与抗干扰鲁棒性之间的矛盾，是一种实用的人形机器人运动控制方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the prevailing approach to humanoid locomotion control: policies transfer reliably from simulation to hardware and recover gracefully from disturbances.
  Motion quality, however, still lags behind: task-only rewards often converge to stiff, asymmetric gaits, while motion imitation methods improve appearance but become more sensitive to external disturbances because reference signals can oppose the transient poses needed to regain balance.
  We propose Predictive Style Matching, in which an offline predictor maps the robot's lower-body state history and velocity commands to interpretable upper-body joint and gait targets that shape the rewards during training.
  Because the targets are state-conditioned rather than time-indexed and the predictor is used only at training time, the deployed controller inherits the proprioceptive interface and inference cost of a task-only RL baseline.
  On the Unitree G1, in both simulation and hardware, PSM reduces upper-body style error by roughly an order of magnitude over task-only RL while preserving its fall-recovery rate, whereas the motion-imitation baseline attains the lowest style error but fails to recover from disturbances about five times as often.

</details>


### [118] [Dreaming when Necessary: Advancing World Action Models with Adaptive Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07089)
*Yinzhou Tang,Jingbo Xu,Yu Shang,Zihao Song,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaWAM通过轻量级动态路由器实现自适应多模态推理，在长周期复杂任务中显著提升推理效率并超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型依赖视频预测作为动作先验，缺乏自适应多模态推理能力，在处理长周期复杂任务时效果受限。

Method: 提出AdaWAM，集成轻量级动态路由器，在执行过程中根据上下文自动触发文本推理（任务过渡阶段）或视觉推理（精细操作阶段）。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的具身任务上，AdaWAM显著提升了推理效率，并优于最先进的具身策略。

Conclusion: 自适应多模态推理能有效提升世界动作模型在长周期复杂任务中的性能与效率。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising approach to embodied intelligence, yet existing methods rely heavily on video prediction as action priors and lack adaptive multimodal reasoning, limiting their effectiveness on long-horizon, complex tasks. We observe that WAMs require different multimodal reasoning modes under different execution contexts: textual reasoning is essential during task transitions to guide high-level action prediction, while visual reasoning is critical during fine-grained manipulation for precise control. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{AdaWAM}, a world action model with adaptive multimodal reasoning abilities. AdaWAM integrates a lightweight dynamic router that autonomously triggers textual or visual reasoning as needed during task execution. Experiments on both simulated and real-world embodied tasks show that AdaWAM substantially improves inference efficiency while outperforming state-of-the-art embodied policies. Codes and demos are available at: https://adawam.github.io/.

</details>


### [119] [Coarse-to-Control: Action-Token Planning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07107)
*Jinhao Wu,Shiduo Zhang,Yicheng Liu,Xiaopeng Yu,Sixian Li,Siyin Wang,Hang Zhao,Jing Huo,Yang Gao,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Coarse-to-Control（C2C）框架，在VLA模型中引入原生动作词元空间规划，先预测粗粒度动作词元摘要未来轨迹，再生成可执行动作词元，在长程任务中显著优于直接动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型直接观测到动作的映射缺乏中间规划，早期错误在长程任务中会累积放大，导致性能受限。

Method: 在统一离散动作词元空间中，先让策略预测紧凑的粗粒度动作词元序列（总结未来轨迹），再基于此计划生成可执行的动作词元。计划与执行共享动作词汇表，保持计划的直接可执行性。

Result: 在LIBERO、SimplerEnv-WidowX和真实世界操作任务上，动作词元规划一致优于直接动作生成，长程多阶段任务提升最大。

Conclusion: 在动作词元空间内进行原生规划能有效提升VLA模型的长程任务表现，且计划紧贴控制流形，无需额外的抽象-执行翻译步骤。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models map observations directly to actions without explicit intermediate planning, which limits performance on long-horizon tasks where early mistakes compound. We propose Coarse-to-Control, a plan-execute VLA that introduces planning natively in the action-token space. The key idea is to let the policy first predict a compact sequence of coarse action tokens that summarize the intended future trajectory, and then generate executable action tokens conditioned on this plan. Because both planning and execution share a unified discrete action vocabulary, the plan stays close to the control manifold and provides directly actionable guidance rather than an abstract hint that must be translated back to motor commands. Experiments on LIBERO, SimplerEnv-WidowX, and real-world manipulation tasks show that action-token planning consistently improves over direct action generation, with the largest gains on long-horizon multi-stage tasks.

</details>


### [120] [QuadVerse: An Integrated Framework Aligning Visual-Physical Reality for Quadruped Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07118)
*Yuxiang Chen,Yuanhao Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Yu Liu,Yufei Jia,Tiancai Wang,Erjin Zhou,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuadVerse是一个利用重建场景作为校准基底的统一框架，通过3DGS重建、接触校准和残差动力学补偿，弥合仿真与现实之间的视觉、物理和驱动差距，实现零样本视觉导航策略部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常单独处理视觉或动态的仿真-现实差距，忽略了这些个体不匹配在机器人状态演化中如何积累和传播的问题。

Method: 从RGB视频重建几何约束的3D高斯溅射场景，支持批量逼真自视渲染和碰撞就绪语义网格提取；通过轨迹后验搜索初始化并细化空间变化的摩擦先验进行接触校准；训练残差动力学补偿器，通过在接触校准地形上重放真实轨迹来解耦接触误差与执行器非理想性。

Result: QuadVerse在重建质量和运动跟踪方面优于相关基线，并实现了鲁棒的零样本视觉导航策略部署，无需特定任务的真实世界 rollout。

Conclusion: 通过统一校准视觉感知、物理交互和执行器动力学，QuadVerse有效弥合了sim-to-real差距，为机器人学习提供了更可靠的仿真基础。

Abstract: Simulation is central to robot learning, yet the sim-to-real gap remains a major bottleneck.Existing approaches often tackle visual or dynamic gaps separately, overlooking how these individual mismatches accumulate and propagate throughout the robot's state evolution.In this paper, we introduce QuadVerse, an integrated framework that uses reconstructed scenes as a calibration substrate for aligning visual perception, physical interaction, and actuator dynamics.From captured RGB videos, we reconstruct geometry-constrained 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes that support batched photorealistic ego-view rendering and collision-ready semantic mesh extraction. The meshes further enable contact calibration by initializing spatially varying friction priors and refining them through trajectory-based posterior search.To address remaining actuator discrepancies, QuadVerse trains a residual dynamics compensator by replaying real-world trajectories on the contact-calibrated terrain, reducing the entanglement between terrain-induced contact errors and actuator non-idealities.Experiments show that QuadVerse improves reconstruction quality and locomotion tracking over relevant baselines.Leveraging this foundation, we demonstrate robust zero-shot visual-navigation policy deployment without task-specific real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [121] [Test-Time Trajectory Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07170)
*Yihong Xu,Eloi Zablocki,Yuan Yin,Elias Ramzi,Ellington Kirby,Alexandre Boulch,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TOAD是一种即插即用的方法，通过在测试时使用交叉熵方法对规划器生成的轨迹进行优化搜索，以最大化学习到的轨迹级奖励函数，从而显著提升多个自动驾驶规划器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端规划器先生成候选轨迹再评分，但评分器无法影响轨迹集合的生成质量，弱候选集会限制规划性能。

Method: 将评分器视为学习到的轨迹级奖励函数，在测试时使用交叉熵方法（CEM）进行轨迹搜索，并以规划器原本的轨迹提议作为热启动。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1（94.7 PDMS）、NAVSIM-v2（56.3 EPDMS）和闭环HUGSIM基准测试中，TOAD在六个基础规划器上均取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: TOAD是一种无需重新训练、即插即用的方法，通过测试时优化搜索有效解决了传统规划器中评分器与生成器脱节的问题。

Abstract: End-to-end planners for autonomous driving typically generate a set of candidate trajectories, score each one, and return the highest-scoring candidate. However, the scorer is applied only after the proposals are generated and cannot influence the set of trajectories: a weak set of candidates limits planning performance regardless of the scorer's quality. We instead treat the scorer as a learned trajectory-level reward function and search for trajectories that maximize it. Our method, TOAD, runs the Cross-Entropy Method at test time, warm-started from the planner's proposals. It requires no retraining and is plug-and-play for existing planners. Across six base planners, TOAD improves results on NAVSIM-v1 (94.7 PDMS), NAVSIM-v2 (56.3 EPDMS), and the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark. The code will be made publicly available via the project page: https://valeoai.github.io/TOAD/.

</details>


### [122] [A Causal Probabilistic Framework for Perception-Informed Closed-Loop Simulation of Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07186)
*Zhennan Fei,Rickard Johansson,Mikael Andersson,Matthias Eng,Mattias Eriksson,Kaveh Kianfar,Sadegh Rahrovani,Chris van der Ploeg,Michael Borth,Maren Buermann,Michiel Braat,Henk Goossens,Zijian Han,Majid Khorsand Vakilzadeh,Gabriel Rodrigues de Campos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种感知信息注入的软件在环（SIL）测试方法，通过在标准化仿真环境中引入由物理触发条件（如雾、雨、物体合并）导致的真实感知错误（如漏检、尺寸误差、定位偏移），弥合了理想仿真与现实感知行为之间的差距，揭示了理想SIL环境无法捕获的潜在运行风险，为SOTIF（ISO 21448）验证提供了可扩展的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件在环（SIL）仿真大多采用理想感知模型，绕过了感知算法的功能性缺陷，导致安全评估过于乐观，无法反映真实世界的感知局限性。

Method: 提出感知信息注入的SIL测试框架，将因果概率模型集成到标准化场景仿真工具链中，系统性地注入由物理触发条件（雾、雨、物体合并）导致的真实感知错误（漏检、尺寸误差、定位偏移）。

Result: 感知信息注入的测试能够揭示理想SIL环境无法捕获的潜在运行风险，证明了其在SOTIF验证中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为系统性地评估感知缺陷对安全功能的影响提供了一种可扩展的途径，支持ISO 21448（SOTIF）的验证工作。

Abstract: Software-in-the-loop (SIL) simulation is a cornerstone for the validation of modern automotive safety functions. However, many current frameworks utilize ideal sensing, which bypasses the functional insufficiencies of perception algorithms, leading to over-optimistic safety assessments. This paper proposes a perception-informed SIL testing methodology that bridges the gap between ground-truth simulation and real-world perception behavior. We present a framework for incorporating causal probabilistic models into standardized, scenario-based simulation toolchains, applicable to both Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS) and Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS). Our approach enables the systematic injection of realistic perception errors, such as loss of detection, sizing inaccuracies, and positioning offsets, derived from physical triggering conditions like fog, rain, and object-merging scenarios. By evaluating these ``faults'' within a standardized simulation environment, we demonstrate that perception-informed testing reveals latent operational risks that ideal SIL environments fail to capture, providing a scalable pathway for SOTIF (ISO 21448) validation.

</details>


### [123] [Shield-Loco: Shielding Locomotion Policies with Predictive Safety Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07193)
*Aditya Shirwatkar,Sebastian Sanokowski,Shishir Kolathaya,Aaron Johnson,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种预测性安全滤波器，通过在强化学习策略前过滤接触位置，利用基于全物理模型的采样优化器异步搜索更安全的接触序列，并借助学习到的价值函数引导长期回报，在四足机器人上实现了密集杂乱环境中的安全运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略在动态腿式运动中没有机制避免训练中未出现的约束违反；大规模离线安全学习不切实际；现有安全框架要么依赖降阶模型（无法推理全身行为），要么依赖保守恢复控制器（降低任务性能）。

Method: 提出预测性安全滤波器，后验过滤RL策略的名义接触位置；碰撞预测触发时，基于全物理模型的采样优化器异步搜索更安全接触序列；学习价值函数引导长期回报；三个关键组件：采样接触的几何投影、动量增强更新、副本交换。

Result: 在四足机器人上，仿真和真实世界的密集杂乱环境实验中，显著减少了安全违规，对名义输入的偏离最小。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持任务性能的同时有效提升了安全性，为腿式机器人在复杂环境中的安全运动提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies enable dynamic legged locomotion but lack mechanisms to avoid violations of safety constraints that are absent during training. Large-scale offline safe learning is impractical for covering all edge cases. Existing safety frameworks either rely on reduced-order models that cannot reason about whole-body behaviors or require conservative recovery controllers that degrade task performance. We propose a predictive safety filter that post-hoc filters the nominal contact locations fed to the RL policy. When a collision is predicted, a sampling-based optimizer asynchronously searches for safer contact sequences using a full-physics model, while a learned value function bootstraps long-horizon returns. Our three algorithmic components (geometric projection of sampled contacts, momentum-augmented updates, and replica-exchange) make the optimization tractable in a discontinuous contact landscape. We validate the filter on a quadruped robot in dense, cluttered environments, both in simulation and in the real world, showing substantial reductions in safety violations with minimal deviation from the nominal input.

</details>


### [124] [An Abstract Architecture for Explainable Autonomy in Hazardous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07211)
*Matt Luckcuck,Hazel M Taylor,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持自主系统解释自身行为的抽象架构（可解释自主性），旨在增强用户对自主机器人系统的信任，并以民用核工业为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危险环境中使用自主机器人系统时，人类操作员仍需与其协作，但系统缺乏可解释性会导致信任不足。现有文献表明可解释性与系统可信度密切相关，因此需要将可解释性作为系统设计的一等公民来考虑。

Method: 提出了一种抽象的架构设计模板，用于支持自主系统对其行为进行解释（即可解释自主性），该架构应像安全性和保密性一样被内建于系统中。

Result: 给出了一个在民用核工业中的应用实例，展示了该架构如何帮助工人和监管机构信任系统的决策能力。

Conclusion: 可解释性应被设计进自主系统中，而非事后添加；所提出的抽象架构为构建可解释的自主系统提供了设计模板。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic systems are being proposed for use in hazardous environments, often to reduce the risks to human workers. In the immediate future, it is likely that human workers will continue to use and direct these autonomous robots, much like other computerised tools but with more sophisticated decision-making. Therefore, one important area on which to focus engineering effort is ensuring that these users trust the system. Recent literature suggests that explainability is closely related to how trustworthy a system is. Like safety and security properties, explainability should be designed into a system, instead of being added afterwards. This paper presents an abstract architecture that supports an autonomous system explaining its behaviour (explainable autonomy), providing a design template for implementing explainable autonomous systems. We present a worked example of how our architecture could be applied in the civil nuclear industry, where both workers and regulators need to trust the system's decision-making capabilities.

</details>


### [125] [Robotic Policy Adaptation via Weight-Space Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07217)
*Christian Bianchi,Siamak Yousefi,Alessio Sampieri,Andrea Roberti,Luca Rigazio,Fabio Galasso,Luca Franco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WIZARD提出了一种权重空间元学习框架，通过单次前向传播为冻结的VLA策略生成任务特定的LoRA参数，无需任务特定的动作标签或微调，在LIBERO基准测试中性能提升高达14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型适应新任务仍需任务特定的演示、动作标注和额外微调，部署成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 提出WIZARD权重空间元学习框架：给定语言指令和短视频演示，单次前向传播预测对应LoRA适应权重，无需目标任务动作标签或测试时优化；元训练阶段学习将任务证据映射到专家LoRA更新。

Result: LIBERO基准上：未见数据集集合性能提升约2倍，未见任务性能提升约14倍；真实Franka Emika Panda机器人上持续优于基线。

Conclusion: 生成的适配器能够提供超越仿真的任务级特化，实现了无需任务特定微调的高效VLA模型适应。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation, enabling general-purpose policies trained from large corpora of demonstrations and action labels. However, adapting these models to new tasks still typically requires task-specific demonstrations, action annotations, and additional fine-tuning, making deployment costly and difficult to scale.
  We propose WIZARD, a weight-space meta-learning framework that sidesteps task-specific fine-tuning by generating task-specific LoRA parameters for a frozen VLA policy. Given only a language instruction and a short demonstration video, WIZARD predicts the corresponding adaptation weights in a single forward pass, without target-task action labels or test-time optimization. During meta-training, WIZARD learns to map task evidence directly to expert LoRA updates, capturing relationships between tasks in weight space.
  Experiments on LIBERO show that WIZARD improves performance by up to ~2x on unseen dataset collections and up to ~14x on unseen tasks. On a Franka Emika Panda, WIZARD consistently improves over a real-domain adapted baseline, showing that generated adapters provide task-level specialization beyond simulation.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Waypoints: A Trajectory-Centric Waypointing Paradigm for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07244)
*Haoxiang Shi,Xiang Deng,Haoyu Zhang,Qiaohui Chu,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹路标范式，用可执行轨迹替代传统孤立路标，通过TSDF引导的扩散策略预测轨迹路标，并设计轨迹增强导航器确保高层规划与低层执行的一致性，在VLN-CE基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLN-CE方法采用"路标预测器+导航器+低层控制器"的三阶段解耦范式，存在路标不可达、规划与控制不一致的问题。为此，本文提出将每个候选路标锚定在可执行轨迹上的新范式。

Method: 1）提出轨迹路标预测器，采用TSDF引导的扩散策略生成轨迹，避开障碍物保证路标可达性；2）提出轨迹增强导航器，将关联轨迹作为额外信息注入规划过程，实现高层语义决策与低层执行的严格一致性。

Result: 在VLN-CE基准上进行大量实验，所提轨迹路标范式在各项指标上均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将路标从孤立点扩展为可执行轨迹，能有效解决解耦范式中的不可达和不一致问题，为VLN-CE任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to follow natural-language instructions while navigating in real-world-like environments. Most VLN-CE approach\-es adopt a three-stage framework: a waypoint predictor proposes navigable waypoints, and a navigator selects the best waypoint, with a low-level controller executing the movement to it. However, this decoupled paradigm often leads to unreachable waypoints or inconsistencies between planning and control. In this work, instead of predicting isolated waypoints, we introduce a novel paradigm called Trajectory Waypoint, which grounds each candidate waypoint in an executable trajectory. To realize this, we design a Trajectory Waypoint Predictor formulated as a TSDF-guided diffusion policy, which steers trajectory generation away from obstacles, inherently ensuring the reachability of the predicted waypoints. We further propose a trajectory-enhanced navigator that injects the associated trajectory as additional information for planning, enabling strict consistency between high-level semantic decisions and low-level execution. Extensive experiments on the VLN-CE benchmark show that our Trajectory Waypoint paradigm achieves superior performance over the baselines.

</details>


### [127] [CAPE: Contrastive Action-conditioned Parallel Encoding for Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07304)
*Cong Chen,Haowen Wang,Zhixiang Zhang,Pei Ren,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPE是一个对比式动作条件并行编码框架，通过区分不同动作序列引发的未来结果来学习视觉动态，显著提升了规划效率并降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉动态模型在预测时关注视觉显著性内容而非与规划相关的动作条件变化，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出CAPE框架，采用对比式动作条件并行编码，单次前向传播解码完整未来潜在轨迹，并使用目标收敛对比损失进行训练。

Result: 在真实世界DROID数据集和零样本迁移到RoboCasa上，CAPE在状态检索、动作匹配和闭环规划方面显著优于基线，同时大幅降低长预测步长下的推理成本。

Conclusion: CAPE通过聚焦动作条件变化而非视觉显著性内容，有效提升了具身智能体的规划效率。

Abstract: Embodied agents need to predict the future consequences of candidate actions in order to plan effectively before execution. Existing visual dynamics models learn by reconstructing future visual states or rolling out dense latent representations, which spreads learning capacity across visually salient but planning-irrelevant content rather than the action-conditioned changes that drive manipulation outcomes. We propose CAPE, a Contrastive Action-conditioned Parallel Encoding framework that learns visual dynamics by distinguishing the future outcomes induced by different action sequences. Given an initial observation and a candidate action sequence, CAPE decodes the full future latent trajectory in a single forward pass and is trained with a Goal-Convergent Contrastive Objective that aligns predictions corresponding to the same future outcome while separating those corresponding to different outcomes. On real-world DROID and zero-shot transfer to RoboCasa, CAPE substantially outperforms prior baselines on future-state retrieval, offline action matching, and closed-loop planning, while notably reducing planning-time inference cost at long prediction horizons.

</details>


### [128] [RhinoVLA Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07383)
*Huixi Intelligence,:,Chen Zhang,Chenyang Zhou,Guanglei Ding,Guanghui He,Haibin Gao,Jiajia Chen,Jianyong Zhang,Lianyi Yu,Ningyi Xu,Ping Xu,Qingchen Li,Yingjun Hu,Yijia Zhang,Yuxi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RhinoVLA是一种面向边缘硬件部署的VLA模型，通过token高效骨干、连续动作专家和统一跨机器人接口，在Huixi R1 SoC上实现了11.69 Hz的实时推理，性能媲美π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在边缘硬件上实时部署困难，主要瓶颈在于VLM的视觉和上下文token导致投影算子计算量随token数线性增长，亟需一种部署友好的模型架构。

Method: 采用token高效的Qwen3-VL骨干和连续Action Expert减少VLM侧token与计算负担；引入View Registry、72D物理状态-动作槽空间和机器人实例LoRA的统一接口实现跨机器人学习；通过硬件感知编译、混合精度执行和并行视觉编码优化部署。

Result: 在类似参数规模下实现与π0.5相当的下游性能；在Huixi R1上达到11.69 Hz端到端推理，满足10 Hz实时闭环控制目标。

Conclusion: RhinoVLA证明了通过模型-硬件协同设计可以有效解决VLA模型在边缘设备上的实时部署难题，同时保持良好的任务性能和跨机器人泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation, but real-time deployment on edge hardware remains challenging. In this work, we identify VLM visual and context tokens as a major source of deployment latency: for GEMM-dominated projection operators, computation grows linearly with the number of input tokens when model dimensions are fixed. Motivated by this observation, we propose RhinoVLA, a deployment-oriented VLA model co-designed with the Huixi R1 edge SoC. RhinoVLA adopts a token-efficient Qwen3-VL backbone and a continuous Action Expert, reducing the VLM-side token and computation burden while preserving pretrained multimodal capability. To support cross-robot learning, RhinoVLA further introduces a unified interface that combines View Registry, 72D physical state-action slot space, and robotinstance LoRA, allowing heterogeneous robot observations and action schemas to be aligned under a shared policy. On the deployment side, RhinoVLA is optimized through hardware-aware compilation, mixed-precision execution, and parallel visual encoding. Experiments show that RhinoVLA achieves downstream performance comparable to π0.5 at a similar parameter scale, while reaching 11.69 Hz end-to-end inference on Huixi R1, meeting the 10 Hz real-time closedloop control target. The project will be open-sourced at https://github.com/HuixiAI/RhinoVLA.

</details>


### [129] [Spline Policy: A Structured Representation for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07386)
*Mengze Tian,Yiming Li,Sichao Liu,Auke Ijspeert,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Spline Policy (SP)，一种用样条参数替代固定分辨率动作块的结构化表示方法，能在保持策略骨干不变的同时，提供连续轨迹解码、时间重采样、局部修正、不确定性传播和多控制器兼容等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习策略使用固定分辨率动作块，虽简单有效但缺乏几何和时间结构信息，无法灵活适应不同控制需求。

Method: 将动作块替换为样条参数，支持四次样条输出，通过解析距离场构造转换成状态依赖向量场，在规则性和投影假设下提供局部修正机制，支持不确定性传播和经典控制机制融合。

Result: 在低维运动学习、模拟操作、灵巧操作和真实机器人案例中，SP与多种现代策略骨干（扩散、流匹配、Transformer、VLA）兼容，并展示了紧凑解码、时间重采样、局部修正、不确定性评估和控制器兼容性等优势。

Conclusion: 样条参数化是一种有效的动作表示方式，能够在不改变策略架构的前提下赋予机器人学习策略更多结构化运动特性，为策略与经典控制的结合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern imitation-learning policies for robot manipulation often represent actions as fixed-resolution action chunks, which are simple and effective but expose limited geometric and temporal structure before execution. This paper studies Spline Policy (SP), a structured representation that replaces action chunks with spline parameters while keeping the policy backbone unchanged. The predicted spline can be decoded as a compact continuous trajectory, queried at different temporal resolutions, constrained or edited in parameter space, and passed to downstream controllers. For quadratic spline outputs, the same representation can also be converted into a state-dependent vector field through an analytical distance-field construction. Under the regularity and projection assumptions of this construction, the induced dynamics do not increase the distance to the generated spline, yielding a principled local corrective mechanism around the predicted motion. The spline output further supports uncertainty propagation from observations to spline parameters, trajectories, and flow fields, and can be combined with classical control mechanisms such as null-space collision avoidance without retraining the policy backbone. We instantiate SP with diffusion, flow-matching, transformer-based, and vision-language-action backbones. Experiments in low-dimensional motion learning, simulated manipulation under matched backbones, dexterous manipulation, and real-robot case studies show that SP remains compatible with modern policy learners while exposing useful motion-structure properties, including compact decoding, temporal resampling, local correction around predicted motions, uncertainty evaluation, and controller compatibility.

</details>


### [130] [Simulation-Driven Imitation Learning for Biosignals-Free Shared-Autonomy Prosthetic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07389)
*Kaijie Shi,Wanglong Lu,Huiling Chen,Vinicius Prado da Fonseca,Ting Zou,Hanli Zhao,Xianta Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的仿真框架，用于生成多样化机器人假肢抓取演示数据，通过模仿学习实现无需生物信号的共享自主控制，仿真到真实迁移成功率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模仿学习的假肢共享自主控制方法依赖大量真实人类演示数据，数据采集成本高且变异性大，限制了方法的可扩展性。

Method: 构建可扩展仿真框架，自动生成从腕部虚拟摄像头视角的抓取演示，包含物理可行的抓取合成、自然运动轨迹重定向，以及程序化生成室内环境中的抓取-提起执行流程，记录腕部视觉观测、本体感受和动作数据用于模仿学习。

Result: 在三种真实场景中，学习到的仿真到真实策略实现了超过90%的抓取成功率，超越了基线方法，并展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 仿真驱动的训练方法对于无需生物信号的共享自主假肢抓取具有显著潜力，可以为策略学习提供足够丰富和一致的演示数据。

Abstract: Biosignals-free shared-autonomy control of upper-limb prosthetic hands aims to enable natural and low-effort manipulation without relying on EMG or other physiological signals. Recent imitation-learning-based approaches have shown promising results, but their scalability is limited by the cost and variability of collecting large amounts of real-world human demonstration data. In this work, we present a scalable simulation framework that automatically generates diverse reach-to-grasp demonstrations from a wrist-mounted virtual camera. The framework combines physically feasible grasp synthesis, natural reaching trajectories retargeting, and reach--grasp--lift execution in procedurally generated indoor environments. It records wrist-view observations, proprioception, and actions to build a large-scale demonstration dataset for imitation learning. Through extensive simulation benchmarks, we evaluate object and scene generalization and compare several representative state-of-the-art imitation learning methods. Results show that the simulated demonstrations are sufficiently rich and consistent for effective policy learning. In three realistic settings, the learned sim-to-real policy achieves over 90\% grasp success, surpasses baseline methods, and exhibits stronger generalization, highlighting the promise of simulation-driven training for biosignals-free shared-autonomy prosthetic grasping. The demonstrations are available at \href{https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home}{https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home}.

</details>


### [131] [Rapid co-design of Buoyancy-assisted robots for Challenging Locomotion using Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07424)
*Ankit Sinha,Nitish Sontakke,Dennis Hong,Yusuke Tanaka,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists (GES)框架，通过解耦设计空间划分与策略学习，将专家策略分配给演化中的高斯区域，解决了机器人形态-控制协同设计中通用策略行为多样性崩溃的问题，在BALLU机器人上实现了5-25%的性能提升、3倍越障高度提升和37%的设计优化时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型无关强化学习虽可用于机器人形态优化，但协同设计内循环中反复训练策略成本高昂；通用策略虽能加速但存在行为多样性崩溃问题，导致在所有设计上表现次优；端到端MoE架构则因表示崩溃而失败。

Method: 提出Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists (GES)框架，将设计空间划分为高斯区域，为每个区域分配专门的专家策略，通过训练、探测和领地扩展三个步骤迭代优化。专家策略集成到设计采样循环中，替代昂贵的重新训练，直接进行评估。

Result: 在BALLU机器人上，GES发现的设计性能比通用策略高5-25%；硬件实验中优化设计可跨越24cm高障碍物，比基线提升3倍；设计优化时间减少37%。

Conclusion: GES通过显式捕获多样化行为，有效解决了协同设计中策略训练的效率和性能问题，在降低计算成本的同时显著提升了设计质量。

Abstract: Designing high-performance legged robots requires jointly optimizing morphology and control. Model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers an alternative to model-predictive control for developing robust controllers without explicitly specifying robot dynamics. Thus, we have seen theuse of RL to train controllers and evaluate designs for robot morphology optimization. While RL has shown success inlocomotion, using it in the co-design inner loop is expensive due to repeated policy training. Universal policies conditioned on morphology offer a promising alternative, but suffer from behavioral diversity collapse, converging to a single strategy that performs sub-optimally across designs. On the other hand, end-to-end Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures fail due to a collapse in its representation. We propose Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists (GES), a framework that decouples design-space partitioning from policy learning to capture diverse behaviors explicitly. GES assigns specialist policies to evolving Gaussian regions and iteratively refines them via training, probing, and territory expansion. The resulting specialists are integrated into a design sampling loop, replacing costly re-training with direct evaluation. When tested on the Buoyancy-Assisted Light Legged Unit (BALLU), GES discovers designs with 5 - 25% higher performance than naive universal policies. On hardware, a GES optimized design overcomes a 24 cm tall obstacle - 3x improvement over the baseline BALLU design. Moreover, GES curtails design optimization time by 37%.

</details>


### [132] [Re-imagining ISO 26262 in the Age of Autonomous Vehicles: Enhancing Controllability through Transferability and Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07437)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Paul Schmitt,Adam Shoemaker,Bodo Seifert,Steve Kenner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文针对ISO 26262标准在自动驾驶（AV）场景下缺乏人类驾驶员的问题，将"可控性（Controllability）"分解为两个可量化的子概念：可迁移性（Transferability）和可预测性（Predictability）。前者衡量AV将控制权移交给安全机制的能力，后者量化外部主体预测AV行为的能力。论文建立了数学模型，并引入了设计能力与可实现能力之间的差距概念，使得回退和交互声明在ODD切片上可证伪、可追溯。该扩展与ISO 26262和ISO/PAS 21448（SOTIF）兼容，适用于L4/L5自动驾驶系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO 26262标准基于人类驾驶范式定义功能安全，但在完全自动驾驶（SAE L4/L5）中缺少人类驾驶员，原有的"可控性（Controllability）"概念不再适用。现有标准无法有效评估无人驾驶系统的回退能力和交互安全性，亟需一种可审计、可量化的扩展方法。

Method: 将Controllability分解为Transferability（系统将控制权转移给回退安全机制的能力）和Predictability（外部主体预测AV行为的难易程度）。Predictability基于人机交互原理定义，并建立数学量化框架。引入"设计能力vs可实现能力"差距（designed-versus-achievable gap），区分架构层面的回退声明与场景条件下的实际回退能力。

Result: 提出的度量指标与ISO 26262和ISO/PAS 21448（SOTIF）标准兼容，使回退声明和交互声明在不同ODD（运行设计域）切片上变得可证伪、可追溯。该框架保持了ISO 26262的结构完整性，同时扩展了其对无人驾驶系统的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架是对现有标准的补充而非替代，通过引入可迁移性和可预测性两个可审计维度，有效弥补了ISO 26262在L4/L5自动驾驶安全评估中的空白，使得功能安全评估在无人驾驶场景下依然具有可操作性和可追溯性。

Abstract: The ISO 26262 standard defines functional safety for road vehicles through risk assessments based on Severity, Exposure, and Controllability, grounded in a human-driven vehicle paradigm. In the context of autonomous vehicles (AVs), the absence of a human driver necessitates revisiting these principles. This paper decomposes the Controllability placeholder into two auditable evidence dimensions of ISO 26262 by introducing two measurable sub-concepts: Transferability and Predictability. Transferability extends Controllability to capture AV systems' ability to hand off control to dedicated fallback safety mechanisms, while Predictability captures how easily external agents can anticipate AV behavior. Predictability is formally defined from human-robot interaction-inspired principles, and a mathematical framework is provided to quantify it. A designed-versus-achievable gap is introduced to distinguish architectural fallback claims from scene-conditioned achievable fallback capability. The proposed metrics align with ISO 26262 and ISO/PAS 21448 (SOTIF), rendering fallback and interaction claims falsifiable and traceable across ODD slices. These dimensions complement rather than replace existing standards, and the enhancements preserve the structure of ISO 26262 while extending its applicability to driverless automated systems operating at SAE Levels 4 and 5.

</details>


### [133] [Planning-aligned Token Compression for Long-Context Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07464)
*Zhixuan Liang,Yuxiao Chen,Yurong You,Peter Karkus,Wenhao Ding,Boyi Li,Alexander Popov,Yan Wang,Maximilian Igl,Yiming Li,Danfei Xu,Nikolai Smolyanskiy,Boris Ivanovic,Ping Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COMPACT-VA，一種基於條件VQ-VAE的規劃對齊工作記憶框架，通過壓縮擴展時序上下文為有界表示，在保持決策關鍵信息的同時實現3.3倍速度提升和2.7倍內存減少，成功率提升6%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 單體視覺-動作模型在處理擴展時序上下文時，token序列快速超出實時計算預算。現有的token壓縮方法採用基於規則的啟發式方法（如時間衰減），與規劃解耦，可能丟失決策關鍵信息。

Method: 基於條件VQ-VAE的規劃對齊工作記憶框架。壓縮條件化於歷史軌跡和學習的規劃意圖（後驗編碼器從未來軌跡蒸餾，先驗編碼器從壓縮觀測預測）。壓縮記憶與預測潛變量拼接輸入策略網絡進行端到端優化。

Result: 在可比token預算下，成功率提升6%以上（達68.3%），各指標一致提升。閉環評估中，相比未壓縮處理實現3.3倍加速和2.7倍內存減少，同時保持一般駕駛性能。

Conclusion: 規劃對齊的耦合壓縮有效保留決策關鍵信息，驗證了該方法的有效性。

Abstract: Monolithic vision-action models represent an emerging paradigm in autonomous driving. However, this architecture produces token sequences that quickly exceed real-time computational budgets when encoding extended temporal context for complex interactions. While approaches like linear transformers and external memory try to make the context lightweight, token compression is most compatible with the architecture as it requires no backbone modifications. Yet existing compression adopts rule-based heuristics like temporal decay, decoupled from planning, risking loss of decision-critical information. We propose COMPACT-VA, a planning-aligned working memory framework built on conditional VQ-VAE, compressing extended context into bounded representations. Compression is conditioned on both historical trajectory and a learned planning intent that the posterior encoder distills from future trajectories during training, while the prior encoder learns to predict it from compressed observations. The compressed memory, concatenated with the predicted latent, feeds the policy for end-to-end optimization, planning with retained decision-critical information. We evaluate on high-signal dynamic scenarios where historical context is most critical for behavior correctness (e.g., stop, yield, or proceed), and accordingly design behavioral metrics. Under comparable token budgets, we achieve $>$6% improvement (68.3%) on success rates with consistent gains across metrics. Ablations validate planning-aligned coupling effectiveness. Closed-loop evaluation confirms that COMPACT-VA maintained general driving performance with 3.3* speedup and 2.7* memory reduction over uncompressed processing.

</details>


### [134] [Affordance-Based Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadruped Pedipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07506)
*Tuba Girgin,Jose Castelblanco,Gabriel Rodriguez,Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三层分层强化学习框架，利用姿态可供性指导四足机器人的物体操纵，无需预设计轨迹，并在仿真和真实环境中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人操纵研究依赖专家设计的底层策略和高层轨迹，缺乏自主选择交互点和机器人姿态的能力，限制了机器人在复杂任务中的灵活性和泛化性。

Method: 提出三层分层强化学习框架：顶层利用姿态可供性引导导航策略，中层驱动移动策略，底层通过交互点可供性实现末端执行器（腿部）操纵规划，并在IsaacSim中训练。

Result: 仿真中多个场景验证了姿态可供性的有效性；真实环境中成功执行多种物体交互任务，并形成了物体交互数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架能自主识别候选姿态的可供性，无需人工引导即可在真实世界中成功执行物体操纵任务。

Abstract: The object manipulation capabilities of quadruped robots is an open research challenge. While previous studies have focused on low-level policy learning, task execution still relies on expert-designed high-level trajectories. Autonomous selection of both an affordable interaction point on the target object and an affordable robot base pose removes the need for pre-designed trajectories. This study proposes a three-level hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework that utilizes pose affordances to guide the navigation policy, while the navigation policy drives the locomotion policy. In addition, the pedipulation policy is guided by interaction-point affordances, enabling object-centric pose alignment of the quadruped robot and effective end-effector manipulation planning. We train the proposed framework in the IsaacSim ecosystem and evaluate it in both simulation and real-world settings. We investigate the effectiveness of pose affordance across multiple scenarios in simulation while various object interaction tasks are validated on real-world setting forming an object-interaction dataset. The results show that the proposed framework can autonomously identify candidate poses based on their affordance and successfully execute object manipulation tasks in the real world without human guidance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Detecting and Mitigating Bias by Treating Fairness as a Symmetry Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06514)
*Nishit Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将算法公平性视为对称性破缺问题，通过损失正则化恢复对称性，在合成数据集上减少90%以上偏差，仅牺牲约5%的准确率，且无需因果图知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统在高风险社会经济场景中经常表现出偏见，现有公平性方法往往需要因果图知识或计算成本高，缺乏一种轻量级、通用的偏差矫正框架。

Method: 将偏差形式化为对称性破缺操作：公平分类器应在切换敏感属性（保持能力特征不变）的反事实操作下输出不变。通过基于损失的正则化作为对称性恢复机制来实现公平性。

Result: 在四个合成数据集上实现90%以上的违规减少，准确率代价约为5%。

Conclusion: 该框架无需因果图知识，计算轻量，可推广到任何可定义为比特翻转的敏感属性，适用于主流基准测试中未涵盖的本地歧视来源场景。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in high stakes socioeconomic settings routinely display bias. We formalize bias as a symmetry breaking operation: a classifier is fair if its outputs remain invariant under the counterfactual operation of switching a sensitive attribute, with merit features held fixed. We implement loss based regularization as a symmetry restoring mechanism and evaluate the framework on four synthetic datasets with varying levels of noise, correlation, and bias. The framework achieves upwards of 90\% violation reduction, with accuracy costs around 5\%. This framework does not require causal graph knowledge, is computationally lightweight, and generalizes to any sensitive attribute definable as a bit-flip, making it suitable for contexts where local sources of discrimination remain absent from mainstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [DiBS: Diffusion-Informed Branch Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06518)
*Bo Liu,Yuan Xie,Yuan Gao,Xiaolong Luo,Peng Ye,Tao Chen,Fujun Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合扩散模型引导的符号求解器DiBS，用于数独分支选择，在保持求解完整性的同时大幅减少搜索代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数独求解方法中，学习型求解器缺乏硬正确性保证，而完整符号求解器仍易陷入长尾搜索困境，两者互补但各有局限。

Method: DiBS保持符号求解器的完整性，利用扩散模型作为分支排序引导，核心是对当前部分赋值下的候选值进行排序，并结合轻量级一致性信号。

Result: 在Royle 17-clue数独基准上，DiBS相比强启发式基线大幅降低了搜索代价，尤其在节点数、回溯次数和长尾百分位数上表现显著。

Conclusion: 学习到的全局引导在分支顺序错误代价最高的困难实例上非常有效，验证了方法的优越性。

Abstract: Sudoku is a representative constraint satisfaction problem that requires global structural reasoning under strict discrete constraints. The existing works of solving Sudoku mainly focus on two dominant approaches, i.e., traditional heuristic and deep learning solver. However, they suffer from two complementary limitations: learning-based solvers lack hard correctness guarantees, while complete symbolic solvers are still prone to long-tail search. To address these shortcomings, we propose a novel diffusion model-guided approach, termed as DiBS, for the branch selection search process. Specifically, DiBS keeps the symbolic solver complete and uses the diffusion model as a branch-ordering guide. The core method is ranking candidate values under the current partial assignment and lightweight consistency signal. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth theoretical proof to reveal how it works and why it works. Experiments on the challenging Royle 17-clue Sudoku benchmark show that our DiBS substantially reduces search cost relative to strong heuristic baselines, especially in nodes, backtracks, and long-tail percentiles. Besides, these results confirm that learned global guidance is effective on hard instances where branch-order mistakes are most expensive. All codes are available at https://github.com/shanxierdan/DiBS.

</details>


### [137] [SafeGene: Reusable Adapters for Transferable Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06519)
*Yanghan Wang,Zhiqiang Kou,Fu Feng,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeGene是一个可复用的安全适配器模块，通过解耦安全能力与任务特定更新，在跨任务场景下恢复微调后LLM的安全对齐，有效降低有害响应率同时保持下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型在下游微调过程中，即使训练数据不含恶意内容，安全对齐也会被削弱，导致模型易受恶意提示攻击。且模型频繁更新带来反复的安全恢复问题。

Method: 将安全能力建模为独立、可复用的适配器表示（与任务更新解耦）；通过对齐模型与退化模型的差异提取安全表示；通过数据感知的层选择精炼为可迁移的安全向量；通过少样本层系数重校准注入下游任务模型。

Result: 在多个模型家族、下游任务和安全评估器上，SafeGene增强的模型在降低有害响应率的同时保持了下游性能，在安全-效用权衡上优于代表性安全适应方法。

Conclusion: SafeGene通过可复用安全适配器设计，有效解决了微调后LLM安全恢复的重复性问题，实现了跨任务的安全能力迁移与安全-效用的良好平衡。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are increasingly fine-tuned into customized assistants, but downstream fine-tuning can weaken safety alignment and make models more vulnerable to malicious prompts, even when the training data is not intentionally harmful. This creates a recurring safety recovery problem as target models are repeatedly updated with new task data or user interactions. We propose SafeGene, a reusable safety-adapter module designed for cross-task reuse within each architecture-compatible model family. Rather than treating safety recovery as a model-specific repair step, SafeGene treats safety capability as an independent, reusable adapter representation decoupled from task-specific updates. This representation is obtained from aligned--degraded model discrepancies, refined into task-transferable safety vectors through data-aware layer selection, and expressed in each downstream task-adapted model via few-shot layer-wise coefficient recalibration. Experiments across multiple model families, downstream tasks, and safety judges show that SafeGene-enhanced models reduce harmful response rates while maintaining downstream performance, outperforming representative safe adaptation methods in safety--utility trade-off.

</details>


### [138] [Lean4Agent: Formal Modeling and Verification for Agent Workflow and Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06523)
*Ruida Wang,Jerry Huang,Pengcheng Wang,Xuanqing Liu,Luyang Kong,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Lean4Agent框架，首次使用依赖类型形式语言Lean4对LLM智能体工作流进行形式化建模与验证。包含FormalAgentLib库和LeanEvolve进化机制，在SWE-Bench等任务上验证通过的工作流性能平均提升11.94%，LeanEvolve进一步提升7.47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体系统缺乏形式化方法来指定、验证和调试其工作流与执行轨迹，这借鉴了数学中以形式语言克服自然语言歧义性的范式。

Method: 1) FormalAgentLib：基于Lean4依赖类型形式语言的扩展库，用于形式化建模和验证智能体工作流的语义一致性，并定位执行失败点。2) LeanEvolve：利用验证结果自动修正工作流以增强能力。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified和ELAIP-Bench的5个LLM上，验证通过的工作流比失败的工作流平均高11.94%，LeanEvolve进一步提升SWE性能平均7.47%。

Conclusion: Lean4Agent为使用表达性依赖类型形式语言形式化建模和验证智能体行为奠定了基础，开辟了新研究方向。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) to execute reliable multi-step workflows has become a central challenge in artificial intelligence. Despite recent advances in LLMs' agentic capabilities, most agent systems still lack formal methods for specifying, verifying, and debugging their workflow and execution trajectories. This challenge mirrors a long-standing problem in mathematics, where the ambiguity of natural languages (NLs) motivates the development of formal languages (FLs). Inspired by this paradigm, we propose **Lean4Agent**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework that uses Lean4, a dependent-type FL to model and verify agent behavior. **Lean4Agent** launches **FormalAgentLib**, an extensible Lean4 library for formally modeling and verifying agent workflows' semantic consistency under explicit assumptions, and enabling localization of execution-time failures revealed by trajectories. Building on **FormalAgentLib**, we further develop **LeanEvolve**, which applies results in **FormalAgentLib** to revise workflows to enhance its capability. Extensive experiments on a hard problem subset of SWE-Bench-Verified and a subset of ELAIP-Bench across 5 leading LLMs indicate that the verification-passing workflows outperform the failing ones by an average of **11.94%**, and **LeanEvolve** further improves SWE performance by **7.47%** on average. Furthermore, **Lean4Agent** establishes a foundation for a new field of using expressive dependent-type FL to formally model and verify agent behavior.

</details>


### [139] [CrowdMath: A Dataset of Crowdsourced Mathematical Research Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06526)
*Sherin Muckatira,Jesse Geneson,Slava Gerovitch,Pavel Etingof,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrowdMath数据集，包含164个来自MIT PRIMES–AoPS CrowdMath项目的专家标注进展链，用于评估大语言模型在协作开放问题求解场景下的能力。基准测试发现，前沿模型在下一帖预测上表现尚可（83-88%），但在帖子角色分类上效果很差（宏F1仅0.42），揭示了模型在理解协作式数学进展方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准仅评估有明确答案的封闭问题，无法捕捉协作式开放问题求解这一真实场景——参与者需要提出部分论证、识别错误、修复缺陷并逐步整合出完整证明。作者希望填补这一评估空白。

Method: 从MIT PRIMES–AoPS CrowdMath项目（2016-2025）中收集164条多人论坛讨论链，每条链从开放问题到完整证明。由专家标注每条帖子的功能角色（部分进展、证明完成、错误推理、错误识别等），并定义了下一帖预测和帖子角色分类两个评估任务。

Result: 六种前沿模型在下一帖预测任务上达到83-88%准确率，表明模型能把握局部讨论流向。但在帖子角色分类任务上，最佳模型仅获得0.42宏F1分数，说明模型难以识别各贡献在协作过程中的功能意义。

Conclusion: CrowdMath揭示了当前大语言模型在解决明确数学问题与理解协作式数学进展之间存在显著差距，为未来提升模型在开放、协作环境中的推理能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models have made substantial progress on mathematical reasoning, but existing benchmarks typically evaluate well-specified problems with final answers, step-by-step solutions, or complete proofs. They do not capture collaborative open-problem solving: a setting in which participants propose partial arguments, identify gaps or errors in prior steps, repair flawed reasoning, and gradually synthesize incremental contributions into a proof. We introduce CrowdMath, a dataset of 164 expert-annotated progress chains from the MIT PRIMES--Art of Problem Solving (AoPS) CrowdMath program (2016-2025), a collaborative research initiative whose discussions have led to peer-reviewed publications. Each chain traces a multi-participant forum discussion from an open-problem statement to a completed proof. Posts are labeled by their functional roles in the evolving solution process, including partial progress, proof completion, erroneous reasoning, and error identification. We define evaluation tasks and benchmark six frontier models. Models achieve 83-88% accuracy on next-post prediction, suggesting that they can follow the local flow of mathematical discussion. However, they struggle to identify the functional significance of individual contributions with the best model achieving only 0.42 macro-F1 on post-role classification. CrowdMath exposes a gap between solving well-specified mathematical problems and understanding collaborative mathematical progress as it unfolds.

</details>


### [140] [Attack Selection in Agentic AI Control Evaluations Meaningfully Decreases Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06529)
*Catherine Ge-Wang,Tyler Crosse,Benjamin Hadad,Joachim Schaeffer,Ram Potham,Tyler Tracy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI控制评估中的攻击选择能力，即攻击者策略性地选择攻击时机（启动策略）和终止攻击时机（停止策略），发现这两种策略能显著降低安全性（在1%审计预算下最多降低28个百分点），表明现有控制评估可能高估了对选择性攻击者的安全估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制评估通常假设攻击者不会策略性地选择攻击时机，但这种假设与实际情况不符——战略性选择攻击时机的攻击者更难被捕获。因此需要研究攻击选择能力对安全评估的影响。

Method: 将攻击决策分解为启动策略（决定何时开始攻击）和停止策略（决定何时中止正在进行的攻击），在BashArena和LinuxArena两个智能体环境中进行实验评估。

Result: 在1%审计预算下，启动策略使两个环境的安全性降低20个百分点；停止策略使BashArena安全性降低20个百分点，使LinuxArena安全性降低28个百分点。这些降低被视为攻击选择效应的上界。

Conclusion: 现有控制评估可能对选择性攻击者给出过于乐观的安全估计，建议未来的评估、系统卡和安全案例中纳入攻击选择能力，以产生更现实的安全估计。

Abstract: An attacker that strategically chooses when to attack is much harder to catch than one that attacks indiscriminately. AI control is a safety framework for deploying capable but untrusted AI agents under the oversight of a weaker, trusted monitor and a limited human audit budget. Control evaluations stress-test these protocols by pitting a red-team attack policy against the blue-team monitor, but current evaluations typically assume attackers that do not strategically select when to attack. We study this capability, attack selection, in agentic settings by decomposing attack decisions into a start policy, which decides when an attacker should attack, and a stop policy, which decides when an attacker should abort an ongoing attack. Across two agentic settings, BashArena and LinuxArena, both policies substantially lower measured empirical safety without changing the underlying attack capability. At a 1% audit budget, our start policy reduces safety by 20pp on both BashArena and LinuxArena, and our stop policy reduces safety by 20pp on BashArena and 28pp on LinuxArena. These reductions should be interpreted as upper bounds on the effect of attack selection. Existing control evaluations may therefore yield overly optimistic safety estimates against selective attackers. We recommend that future evaluations, system cards, and safety cases elicit attack selection to produce more realistic safety estimates.

</details>


### [141] [CARVE-Q: Quantum-Proposed, Classically Certified Interactive Driving Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06531)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARVE是一个用于交通场景中否决机动动作的认证修复架构，通过构建有限修复格并生成结构化证书来保证修复的合法、可审计和责任有界性。为解决多车辆修复带来的计算瓶颈，引入量子搜索层CARVE-Q，利用量子最小查找将查询复杂度从Θ(M)降至O(√M)，实现量子提议、CARVE认证的可信控制模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测和博弈论规划器无法提供硬规则、路权、成本分配和回退策略的数学证明，导致否决后的修复行为缺乏责任边界、可审计性和合法性保证。

Method: 提出CARVE架构，构建否决机动动作的有限修复格(M = ∏_j |A_j|)；引入CARVE-Q量子搜索层，在保持安全认证完全经典的前提下，仅对黑箱修复格应用Dürr-Hoyer/Grover量子最小查找算法，将查询复杂度从Θ(M)降至O(√M)。

Result: 在Lanelet2-grounded INTERACTION回放实验中，实现100%路权尊重、100%责任一致性、零优先级误报；在最多65,536个分配上验证了量子最小查找的可实现性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种"量子提议、CARVE认证"的可信自主系统控制范式，在保证安全认证权威完全经典的前提下，利用量子AI加速修复搜索过程。

Abstract: The critical question after a correct driving veto is not only whether a maneuver is unsafe, but whether the blocked interaction admits a lawful, auditable, and responsibility-bounded repair. Prediction and game-theoretic planners can suggest plausible cooperation, yet they do not return a proof that the repair respects hard rules, right-of-way, cost allocation, and ego fallback. We introduce CARVE, Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed maneuvers via Envelopes, a certificate architecture for prediction-free interactive repair. Given a vetoed maneuver, CARVE constructs a finite repair lattice and emits a structured certificate recording the binding rule, selected joint repair, right-of-way-scaled cooperation envelope, responsibility-weighted cost split, and ego-only fallback. This certificate view reveals the algorithmic bottleneck: multi-owner repair induces a product lattice $M = \prod_j |\mathcal{A}_j|$. We therefore introduce CARVE-Q, a verifier-shielded quantum-AI search layer that applies quantum minimum finding only to this black-box lattice while leaving all safety authority classical. In the conservative verifier-oracle model, exact classical minimum finding requires $Θ(M)$ queries in the worst case, whereas Durr-Hoyer/Grover minimum finding uses $O(\sqrt{M})$ oracle queries with high probability. We prove verifier-shielded certificate soundness, priority non-elicitation, black-box query separation, and finite-precision reversible-oracle constructibility. We then demonstrate state-vector minimum finding on CARVE repair oracles up to 65,536 assignments and validate certificate preservation on Lanelet2-grounded INTERACTION replay with 100% right-of-way respect, 100% blame consistency, and zero priority false positives. The result is a trust-bounded quantum-AI pattern for certified autonomy: quantum proposes; CARVE certifies.

</details>


### [142] [Position: Don't Just "Fix it in Post": A Science of AI Must Study Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06533)
*Stella Biderman,Mohammad Aflah Khan,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Catherine Arnett,Fazl Barez,Naomi Saphra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI科学必须超越对静态模型的后验分析，转向研究训练动态过程，以理解能力、偏见、鲁棒性和安全性等重要行为为何产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究大多将模型视为固定产物，仅分析训练后的行为，而非追问这些行为在训练过程中如何涌现。这种后验修补的方式无法提供真正的科学理解。

Method: 基于科学历史和哲学提出理论要求，主张建立支持预测、干预和设计的训练动态理论，并在机械可解释性、公平性、记忆化和简单性偏好等方向检验进展。

Result: 指出缩放定律已在损失预测上取得常规成功，但将类似理解扩展到能力、偏见、鲁棒性和安全相关行为仍是核心挑战。

Conclusion: AI科学需要从"描述现象"转向"理解产生机制"，通过研究训练动态实现预测、干预和设计，这是一系列具体的开放性难题。

Abstract: What would it mean to have a scientific understanding of AI? Models are not static objects: they are snapshots of time-evolving processes shaped by data, objectives, architectures, and optimization dynamics. Yet much of AI research treats models as fixed artifacts, analyzing behaviors after training rather than asking why they emerge. This position paper argues that a science of AI must move beyond post-hoc fixes and study the training dynamics that produce model behavior. Such a science should support progressively stronger forms of understanding: predicting outcomes from early training signals, intervening when trajectories go wrong, and ultimately designing training procedures that more reliably produce desired properties. Scaling laws have made prediction routine for loss; the challenge is extending this success to capabilities, biases, robustness, and safety-relevant behaviors. We articulate requirements for such theories grounded in the history and philosophy of science, examine progress in mechanistic interpretability, fairness, memorization, and simplicity bias, and identify concrete open problems.

</details>


### [143] [Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT): Fully Realising a GPU-based Symmetric Pseudo-Boolean SAT Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06641)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AFSAT是一个基于JAX的GPU加速伪布尔可满足性求解器，通过连续局部搜索和自动并行化，在数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率上显著优于前序工作FastFourierSAT，并支持多种约束类型混合与多加速器近线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将FastFourierSAT的概念验证方法工程化为一个完备的求解器，解决其在数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率方面的局限性，并支持异构约束类型和长度的混合问题实例。

Method: 利用JAX编译器实现纯函数组合、自动向量化、自动微分和JIT编译；采用定制化离散傅里叶变换缓解浮点表示和稳定性问题；通过JAX数组分片实现多加速器自动并行化。

Result: 相较于概念验证方法，数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率均有显著提升；在多加速器扩展时实现近线性吞吐量。

Conclusion: AFSAT成功将连续局部搜索的GPU加速求解思路工程化，有效解决了前序工作的关键瓶颈，具备良好的扩展性与实际可用性。

Abstract: We present Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT), a GPU-accelerated solver for pseudo-Boolean satisfiability based on continuous local search (CLS). AFSAT realises the proof-of-concept approach, FastFourierSAT, into a fully-engineered solver supporting any heterogeneous mixture of symmetric constraint types and lengths within a single problem instance. Using the JAX compiler, AFSAT leverages pure function composition, automatic vectorisation, automatic differentiation, and just-in-time (JIT) compilation to perform massively parallel CLS across batches of candidate assignments. We demonstrate substantially improved numerical stability, runtime performance, and memory efficiency over the proof-of-concept. We achieve this by way of identifying and addressing various limitations that arise from memory latency and floating-point representation, as well as leveraging automatic parallelisation and compact representations. The inherent representational and stability limitations of floating point are partially addressed by a tailored discrete Fourier transform implementation. We achieve near-linear throughput when scaling to multiple accelerators via JAX array sharding.

</details>


### [144] [A Study of Parallel Continuous Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06656)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究并行连续局部搜索（CLS）解决对称伪布尔约束的可满足性问题，发现冗余约束会抑制收敛、CLS在混合求解中可快速完成部分赋值、以及局部搜索因鞍点密集目标函数而迅速收敛至稳定解质量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并行连续局部搜索（CLS）作为求解具有对称伪布尔约束的布尔可满足性问题的新方法，旨在利用连续优化技术解决离散SAT问题。

Method: 将n变量PB可满足性问题松弛为n维超立方体上的连续优化问题（可微目标函数），并通过大规模实验研究CLS的收敛行为、冗余约束影响及混合求解潜力。

Result: (i) 冗余约束抑制而非加速收敛；(ii) CLS作为混合求解器中的子求解器可快速完成部分赋值；(iii) 由于目标函数鞍点密集，局部搜索迅速收敛到稳定的解质量分布，额外步骤收益递减。

Conclusion: 研究结果指导了CLS在现代加速硬件上用于SAT问题的实际应用。

Abstract: We study parallel Continuous Local Search (CLS) as a solution approach for Boolean satisfiability problems with symmetric pseudo-Boolean (PB) constraints. Here, the $n$-variable PB-satisfiability problem is relaxed to a continuous optimisation problem with a differentiable objective function on an $n$-dimensional hypercube. For satisfiable instances, the global minimisers of this optimisation problem correspond to satisfying assignments of the SAT problem at hand. We present several novel findings via empirical experiments: (i) redundant constraints can inhibit rather than accelerate convergence; (ii) CLS shows promise as a sub-solver in hybridised settings, quickly completing partial assignments; and (iii) local search rapidly converges to a stable distribution of solution quality (i.e., degree of satisfaction), due to saddle-dense objectives where additional solver steps yield diminishing returns. Our findings inform practical uses of CLS for SAT on modern accelerator hardware.

</details>


### [145] [AEGIS: A Backup Reflex for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06660)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AEGIS是一种选择性升级方法，通过在弱策略的冻结激活上使用轻量级探针检测高风险步骤，仅在需要时切换到更强的策略，从而在长视界机器人操作中恢复更多失败轨迹（LIBERO-Spatial上恢复10.1% vs 盲升级4.6%和随机触发5.1%），且仅激活强策略38%的步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界机器人操作往往逐渐失败：一步不良操作使状态恶化，策略陷入无法恢复的困境。失败往往在发生前就已可见，因此需要一种能够提前检测高风险步骤并选择性干预的方法。

Method: 提出AEGIS方法，包含两个核心组件：1) 在弱策略的冻结网络激活上训练轻量级探针，用于早期检测高风险步骤（在前30%轨迹步骤中读取，AUROC为0.764）；2) 当探针标记高风险步骤时，控制权切换到更强的独立策略，但仅针对需要干预的步骤。采用预注册分析计划，包括条件恢复任务率估计量和明确的终止标准。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial上，AEGIS恢复10.1%的失败轨迹，显著高于预算匹配的盲升级（4.6%）和随机触发安慰剂（5.1%）。统计检验显著：与盲升级相比+5.4pp（p=8.5e-6），与随机触发相比+5.0pp（p=1.0e-4）。探针早期窗口AUROC为0.764，95% CI [0.70, 0.84]。强策略仅激活38%的步骤，说明杠杆在于时机而非计算量。在每臂700个共同随机数episode上确认结果，nA-fail=646。

Conclusion: AEGIS通过轻量级探针实现选择性升级策略，有效恢复了长视界机器人操作中的失败轨迹，证明了时机选择比单纯增加计算量更重要。该方法在统计上显著优于盲升级和随机触发基线。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation tends to fail gradually: one bad step degrades the state, and the policy spirals into a basin from which it cannot recover. The failure is often visible before it happens. We introduce AEGIS (Activation-probe Early-warning, Gated Inference Switching), a selective escalation method that uses a lightweight probe on a weak policy's frozen activations to detect high-risk steps while there is still time to act. When the probe flags a step, control switches to a stronger separate policy, but only for the steps that need it. On LIBERO-Spatial, AEGIS recovers 10.1% of the trajectories the weak policy alone loses, versus 4.6% for budget-matched blind escalation and 5.1% for a random-trigger placebo. These gains are significant under one-sided exact paired McNemar tests with Holm-Bonferroni adjustment over three pre-registered contrasts: +5.4pp over blind escalation, p=8.5e-6; +5.0pp over random triggering, p=1.0e-4; paired-trajectory bootstrap CIs exclude zero. AEGIS activates the stronger policy on only 38% of steps, so the lever is timing rather than compute. The probe clears its precondition with an early-window AUROC of 0.764, 95% CI [0.70, 0.84], read from the weak-policy path over the first 30% of trajectory steps before any handoff. We pre-register the full analysis plan, including a conditional recovered-task-rate estimand and explicit kill criteria, and confirm the result on 700 common-random-number episodes per arm, with nA-fail=646.

</details>


### [146] [A Geometric Account of Activation Steering through Angle-Norm Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06735)
*Georgii Aparin,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验揭示线性与球形激活控制方法的本质差异在于角度和径向分量的耦合方式，提出应参数化可解释的角度和径向分量而非单一加性系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性激活控制方法存在局限，球形控制方法虽被提出但前提假设（隐藏状态范数不含概念相关信息）未经验证，且两种方法的几何效应差异不明确。

Method: 通过控制实验分离角度和径向分量，在7个语言模型上系统比较线性与球形控制方法对角度对齐和隐藏状态范数的影响。

Result: 1) 概念主要编码在角度结构中，支持球形方法的基本动机；2) 但范数对控制稳定性和下游效应仍然重要；3) 两种方法的主要差异在于角度和径向分量的耦合方式不同。

Conclusion: 激活控制应参数化为可解释的角度和径向分量，而非将二者纠缠的单一加性系数。

Abstract: Linear activation steering has gained popularity as a simple and empirically effective way to control language model behavior. More recently, spherical steering paradigms have been proposed to address limitations of additive interventions, often motivated by the assumption that hidden-state norm does not carry concept-relevant information. In this work, we revisit this assumption through a controlled empirical study designed to disentangle the roles of angular and radial components. We show that steering methods differ mainly in how they couple two geometric effects: changing a token's angular alignment with a concept direction and changing its hidden-state norm. Across seven language models, we find that concepts are represented primarily in angular structure, supporting the motivation for spherical methods, but that norm remains important for the stability and downstream effects of steering. Our results explain why interventions with similar concept-level effects can behave differently, and suggest that activation steering should be parameterized by interpretable angular and radial components of the intervention, rather than by a single additive coefficient that entangles these two effects.

</details>


### [147] [OpenSkill: Open-World Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06741)
*Zhiling Yan,Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Wei Liang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Lifang He,Philip S. Yu,Ran Xu,Xiang Li,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenSkill框架，让智能体在无目标任务监督的情况下，仅通过开放世界的文档、代码库和网页资源，自主构建技能和自我验证信号，实现从零开始的自我进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法假设存在可用的学习循环（如精心策划的技能、成功轨迹或验证信号），但在真实开放世界部署中，这些条件往往都不具备，智能体仅有一个任务提示。因此需要研究如何从零开始构建技能和验证信号。

Method: OpenSkill框架通过以下步骤实现：1）从文档、代码库和网络获取基础知识和验证锚点；2）将其综合为可迁移的技能；3）基于自我构建的虚拟任务（以锚点为基础而非目标答案）来优化技能。开放世界既提供待学习知识，又提供无监督的练习环境。

Result: 在三个基准测试和两个目标智能体上，OpenSkill在满足无监督约束的同时取得了最佳的自动化通过率。分析表明其技能无需特定模型适配即可跨模型迁移，且自我构建的验证器与真实结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 开放世界资源足以替代目标任务的监督信号，实现有效的智能体自我进化，技能和验证器均可从零构建并跨模型泛化。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents requires adaptation after deployment, but existing approaches assume a usable learning loop, such as curated skills, successful trajectories, or verifier signals. Real open-world deployments may provide none of these, offering only a task prompt. In this work, we study open-world self-evolution, where an agent must build both its skills and its own verification signals from scratch, using open-world resources but no target-task supervision. We propose OpenSkill, a framework that bootstraps this loop: it acquires grounded knowledge and verification anchors from documentation, repositories, and the web, synthesizes them into transferable skills, and refines those skills against self-built virtual tasks grounded in the anchors rather than in target answers. The open world thus supplies both the knowledge to be learned and a supervision-independent practice environment, with target-task supervision reserved for final evaluation. Across three benchmarks and two target agents, OpenSkill attains the best automated pass rate while satisfying the no-supervision constraint. Analysis shows its skills transfer across models without model-specific adaptation, and its self-built verifier aligns with ground-truth outcomes despite never accessing them.

</details>


### [148] [AdMem: Advanced Memory for Task-solving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06787)
*Runzhe Wang,Huilin Lu,Shengjie Liu,Li Dong,Jason Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的自适应记忆框架，结合语义、情景和程序记忆，通过多智能体架构实现长期任务的稳健执行和持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作为工具使用智能体时，在需要记忆、组织和复用知识的长期任务中表现有限。之前的记忆方法主要关注事实存储，程序记忆工作虽改善了任务复用，但往往只是重放过去成功经验，没有处理失败案例或考虑在线可扩展性。

Method: 提出一个统一的自动记忆框架，整合语义、情景和程序记忆，采用短期和长期存储的双层设计。多智能体架构包含行动者、记忆和评论家三个智能体，实现自动记忆生成、奖励标注和自适应检索。长期记忆通过基于奖励的评估、合并和剪枝进行管理。

Result: 在多种环境下的实验表明，该方法在长期多轮任务中的鲁棒性和成功率优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 全面且自适应的记忆对于推进基于LLM的智能体具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise as tool-using agents but remain limited in long-horizon tasks that require remembering, organizing, and reusing knowledge. Prior memory approaches aim to resolve the situation, but mainly focus on storing factual information. Recent work on procedural memory improves task reuse, yet often reduces to replaying past successes without addressing failure cases or online scalability. We introduce a unified and automatic memory framework that integrates semantic, episodic, and procedural memory in a bi-level design combining short-term and long-term stores. A multi-agent architecture with actor, memory, and critic agents enables automatic memory generation, reward annotation, and adaptive retrieval. Long-term memory is managed through reward-based evaluation, merging, and pruning, ensuring scalability and continual improvement. Experiments across various environments show that our approach improves robustness and success on long multi-turn tasks compared to existing baselines. This work highlights the importance of comprehensive, adaptive memory for advancing LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [149] [Evidence-Based Intelligent Diagnostic and Therapeutic Visualization System with Large Language Models: Multi-Turn Interaction and Multimodal Treatment Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06869)
*Yunhan Wang,Yuda Wang,Zhiying Tu,Mingqiang Song,Li Song,Kun Li,Dianhui Chu,Bolin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合知识图谱与多模态交互的中医诊断可视化系统，通过四阶段症状匹配、启发式问诊策略和多模态治疗方案展示，显著提升了诊断透明度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助中医诊断工具存在推理过程不透明、交互被动、治疗方案呈现有限等问题，亟需提升辨证论治的透明度和可解释性。

Method: 基于Neo4j知识图谱（241证候、1263症状、2485关系），设计四阶段症状匹配流程（精确/语义/模糊/大语言模型验证）、信息增益驱动遗传算法优化的主动提问策略，以及多模态治疗方案呈现（AI插图、三维经络腧穴模型、循证文献）。

Result: 知识图谱约束减少32%非标准输出；30例配对比较中诊断信任度显著提升（Cohen's d=1.82, p<0.001），五个认知负荷维度中四个改善，循证参考可信度评分（4.21 vs 2.95）。

Conclusion: 该系统通过知识图谱驱动的可视化与多模态交互，增强了中医诊断推理透明度和治疗方案可解释性，为可信AI辅助中医应用提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Aim: Existing AI-assisted traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic tools suffer from opaque reasoning processes, passive interaction, and limited treatment plan presentation. This study proposes a knowledge-enhanced visual diagnostic system to improve the transparency and interpretability of syndrome differentiation and treatment. Methods: The system is built upon a Neo4j knowledge graph comprising 241 syndromes, 1,263 symptoms, and 2,485 relations. It incorporates a four-stage symptom matching pipeline (exact, semantic, fuzzy, and large language model verification), an information gain-driven proactive questioning strategy optimized with genetic algorithms, and a multimodal treatment presentation integrating artificial intelligence-generated illustrations, three-dimensional meridian-acupoint models, and evidence-based literature. Results: Knowledge graph constraints reduced non-standard outputs by 32%. Case studies validated the effectiveness of the interactive workflow across patient self-assessment, clinician-assisted diagnosis, and traditional Chinese medicine education. Automated paired-comparison evaluation across 30 cases further demonstrated significant improvements in diagnostic trust (Cohen's d = 1.82, p < 0.001), reduced cognitive load (improvements in four of five dimensions), and higher credibility of evidence-based references (4.21 vs. 2.95). Conclusions: The proposed system enhances the transparency of traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic reasoning and the interpretability of treatment plans through knowledge graph-driven visualization and multimodal interaction, offering a practical solution for trustworthy artificial intelligence-assisted traditional Chinese medicine applications.

</details>


### [150] [Workflow-to-Skill: Skill Creation via Routing-Workflow-Semantics-Attachments Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06893)
*Yuyang Zhang,Xinyuan Han,Xudong Jiang,Run Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RWSA工作流导向的中间表示和W2S框架，从异构交互轨迹中自动构建高质量技能（Skills），相比摘要和提示基线方法，在行为回放一致性上提升10.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量技能的手动编写成本高昂，而现有方法简单将轨迹摘要化处理，忽略了轨迹碎片化、冗余以及可能遗漏关键安全行为的问题，需要更结构化的自动构建方法。

Method: 提出RWSA（工作流导向中间表示），将技能分解为工作流结构、执行语义和运行时附件；进而提出W2S框架，包含轨迹分割、局部技能草案生成、共享结构对齐、分支协调、冗余压缩等步骤，保留证据和置信度标注。

Result: 在70个技能上的实验表明，W2S相比基于摘要和提示的基线方法，行为回放一致性提升10.5%。

Conclusion: 轨迹应被视为可执行的运行时规范而非可压缩的文本，自动技能构建需要关注结构化分解和关键行为保持。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on Skills to encode procedural knowledge, yet high-quality Skills remain costly to hand-write. This paper studies automatic Skill construction from heterogeneous interaction evidence, including demonstrations, agent trajectories, tool traces, and execution logs. We argue that trace-to-skill construction is not simple summarization tasks, because traces are fragmented, redundant, and may miss rare but safety-critical behaviors. To address this, we introduce RWSA, a workflow-oriented intermediate representation that decomposes Skills into Workflow structure, execution Semantics, and runtime Attachments, capturing task decomposition, control flow, verification, safety, rollback, and state management. Building on RWSA, we propose W2S, a framework that segments traces, induces local Skill drafts, aligns shared structures, reconciles branches, and compresses redundancy while preserving evidence and confidence annotations. Experiments on 70 Skills show that W2S improves behavioral replay consistency by 10.5% over summarization- and prompting-based baselines, highlighting the need to treat traces as executable runtime specifications rather than compressible text.

</details>


### [151] [Declarative Skills for AI Agents in Knowledge-Grounded Tool-Use Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06923)
*M. Danish Lim,I. Danial Bin Sharudin,Wen Han Chen,Cedric Lim,Laura Wynter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了客服工作流中工具型AI智能体的编排机制，比较了声明式智能体（通过自然语言技能文件实现）、命令式智能体（基于程序化状态机）和无脚手架基线智能体。实验表明，检索质量是主要瓶颈；在高质检索下，声明式技能在程序性任务中持续提升准确率并减少编排错误，而命令式状态机效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非结构化知识库上的真实客服工作流中，如何有效编排工具型AI智能体，比较声明式与命令式两种编排范式的优劣。

Method: 将三种智能体形式化为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP）中的策略类别，分析其信息论与结构特性，并在5种语言模型和2种检索机制下进行实证对比。

Result: 检索质量是AI智能体的主导瓶颈；在高质检索下，声明式技能在程序性任务中持续提升准确率并减少编排错误；命令式状态机不可靠，无法稳定提升任务成功率和合规性。

Conclusion: 声明式编排（自然语言技能文件）是比命令式状态机更有效的工具型AI智能体编排范式，但整体性能仍受限于检索质量。

Abstract: We study orchestration mechanisms for tool-using AI agents in realistic customer-service workflows over an unstructured knowledge base.
